// [04/41] DOSYA: src/App.tsx (39 satır)
================================================================================

import React from 'react';
import { NutritionProvider, useNutrition } from './context/NutritionContext';
import { Navbar } from './components/layout/Navbar';
import { RecipeList } from './components/recipes/RecipeList';
import { RecipeWizard } from './components/recipes/RecipeWizard';
import { RecipeDetailModal } from './components/recipes/RecipeDetailModal';
import { FoodsDatabaseView } from './components/foods/FoodsDatabaseView';
import { ExchangeRulesView } from './components/exchanges/ExchangeRulesView';
import { TemplatesView } from './components/templates/TemplatesView';

const MainWorkspace: React.FC = () => {
  const { activeModule } = useNutrition();

  return (
    <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-4 sm:px-6 py-6 font-sans">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto space-y-6">
        {activeModule === 'recipes' && <RecipeList />}
        {activeModule === 'foods' && <FoodsDatabaseView />}
        {activeModule === 'exchanges' && <ExchangeRulesView />}
        {activeModule === 'templates' && <TemplatesView />}
      </div>
    </main>
  );
};

export default function App() {
  return (
    <NutritionProvider>
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#F8F6F0] flex flex-col text-[#1F2B1D] font-sans selection:bg-[#E3ECE0] selection:text-[#273423]">
        <Navbar />
        <div className="flex-1 flex overflow-hidden">
          <MainWorkspace />
        </div>
        <RecipeWizard />
        <RecipeDetailModal />
      </div>
    </NutritionProvider>
  );
}

// [05/41] DOSYA: src/components/booklets/BookletPrintView.tsx (215 satır)
================================================================================

import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { Printer, X, BookOpen, Clock, Sparkles, UserCheck, FileText } from 'lucide-react';
import { RecipeBooklet, Recipe } from '../../types/nutrition';
import { getUnifiedRecipeIngredients } from '../../data/turkishRecipes';
import { printElementStandalone, downloadElementAsHTML } from '../../services/pdfExportService';

interface BookletPrintViewProps {
  booklet: RecipeBooklet; recipes: Recipe[]; onClose: () => void;
}

const THEME_BG: Record<string, string> = {
  amber: 'bg-amber-900', emerald: 'bg-emerald-900', stone: 'bg-stone-900', indigo: 'bg-indigo-900', rose: 'bg-rose-900'
};

export const BookletPrintView: React.FC<BookletPrintViewProps> = ({ booklet, recipes, onClose }) => {
  const bookletRecipes = useMemo(() => {
    const idSet = new Set(booklet.recipeIds);
    return recipes.filter(r => idSet.has(r.id));
  }, [recipes, booklet.recipeIds]);
  const coverBg = THEME_BG[booklet.coverTheme || 'amber'] || THEME_BG.amber;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] bg-slate-900/90 backdrop-blur-md overflow-y-auto flex flex-col items-center p-2 sm:p-4 print:p-0 print:bg-white print:static booklet-printable-root">
      <div className="sticky top-2 z-[101] w-full max-w-4xl bg-slate-900 text-white px-4 py-3 rounded-xl shadow-2xl flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 mb-4 print:hidden border border-slate-700 no-print">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <BookOpen className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-400 shrink-0" />
          <div>
            <span className="font-bold text-sm block leading-tight">{booklet.title}</span>
            <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">Yayın & Baskı Merkezi · {bookletRecipes.length} Tarif</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
          <button type="button" onClick={e => { e.stopPropagation(); printElementStandalone('booklet-printable-content', booklet.title); }} className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-400 text-amber-950 font-bold rounded-lg text-xs shadow-md transition-all cursor-pointer">
            <Printer className="w-4 h-4" /><span>PDF / Yazdır</span>
          </button>
          <button type="button" onClick={e => { e.stopPropagation(); downloadElementAsHTML('booklet-printable-content', booklet.title, 'El_Kitapcigi'); }} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 font-semibold rounded-lg text-xs border border-slate-700 transition-all cursor-pointer">
            <FileText className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /><span>HTML İndir</span>
          </button>
          <button type="button" onClick={e => { e.stopPropagation(); onClose(); }} className="p-2 text-slate-300 hover:text-white rounded-lg bg-slate-800/80 hover:bg-slate-700 border border-slate-700 transition-colors cursor-pointer" title="Pencereyi Kapat">
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div id="booklet-printable-content" className="w-full max-w-4xl bg-white shadow-2xl rounded-2xl overflow-hidden print:shadow-none print:w-full print:max-w-none print:rounded-none">
        <div className={`p-8 sm:p-14 min-h-[600px] flex flex-col justify-between relative text-white ${coverBg} page-break-after print:min-h-screen`}>
          <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-white/10 backdrop-blur-md flex items-center justify-center font-serif text-xl font-bold border border-white/20">C</div>
              <div>
                <span className="block text-xs font-bold tracking-widest uppercase text-amber-200">CHIA NutriPlan</span>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-white/70 font-mono">Klinik Beslenme & Tarif Sistemi</span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <span className="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold bg-white/10 backdrop-blur-md border border-white/20">Kişiye Özel Tarif Kitapçığı</span>
          </div>

          <div className="my-12 space-y-4">
            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-md bg-white/15 text-xs font-medium backdrop-blur-md border border-white/20">
              <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-300" /><span>Diyetisyen Onaylı Özel Seçki</span>
            </div>
            <h1 className="text-3xl sm:text-5xl font-extrabold font-serif tracking-tight leading-tight text-white drop-shadow-xs">{booklet.title}</h1>
            {booklet.subtitle && <p className="text-lg sm:text-xl font-medium text-amber-100/90 font-sans max-w-2xl leading-relaxed">{booklet.subtitle}</p>}
            {booklet.description && <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-white/80 max-w-xl leading-relaxed pt-2 border-t border-white/15">{booklet.description}</p>}
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 pt-6 border-t border-white/20 text-xs">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-white text-slate-900 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-sm shadow-md"><UserCheck className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-900" /></div>
              <div>
                <span className="block font-bold text-sm text-white">{booklet.authorName || 'Dyt. Zeynep Hale Kocaoğlu'}</span>
                <span className="text-[11px] text-amber-200">Uzman Diyetisyen & Beslenme Danışmanı</span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="text-right text-white/70 font-mono text-[11px]">
              <div>{bookletRecipes.length} Özel Tarif</div>
              <div>Tarih: {new Date().toLocaleDateString('tr-TR')}</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="p-8 sm:p-12 border-b border-slate-200 bg-stone-50/50 page-break-after">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6 pb-3 border-b-2 border-slate-900">
            <div><h2 className="text-xl font-bold font-serif text-slate-900">İçindekiler & Reçete Özeti</h2><p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Bu kitapçıkta yer alan reçetelerin genel bakış tablosu</p></div>
            <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded bg-slate-200 text-slate-800">{bookletRecipes.length} Reçete</span>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-3">
            {bookletRecipes.map((recipe, index) => (
              <div key={recipe.id} className="flex items-center justify-between p-3.5 bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-2xs">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <span className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-slate-900 text-white font-bold text-xs flex items-center justify-center font-mono">{index + 1}</span>
                  <div>
                    <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">{recipe.name}</h3>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                      <span>{recipe.category}</span><span>·</span>
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><Clock className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-400" />{recipe.prepTimeMinutes} dk</span>
                      <span>·</span><span className="font-semibold text-emerald-800">{recipe.dietType}</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <span className="inline-block px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-amber-50 text-amber-950 border border-amber-200 font-mono text-xs font-bold">{recipe.exchangeSummary || 'Değişim Profili Var'}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>

          {booklet.notes && (
            <div className="mt-8 p-4 rounded-xl bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 text-amber-950 text-xs">
              <span className="font-bold block mb-1">Diyetisyen Notu:</span>
              <p className="leading-relaxed text-amber-900">{booklet.notes}</p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="divide-y divide-slate-200">
          {bookletRecipes.map((recipe, idx) => {
            const unifiedIngs = getUnifiedRecipeIngredients(recipe);
            return (
              <div key={recipe.id} className="p-8 sm:p-12 space-y-6 bg-white page-break-after print:p-8">
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 pb-4 border-b border-slate-200">
                  <div className="space-y-1">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-amber-100 text-amber-950 font-mono font-bold text-[11px]">Tarif #{idx + 1}</span>
                      <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500">{recipe.category}</span>
                    </div>
                    <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold font-serif text-slate-900">{recipe.name}</h2>
                  </div>
                  <span className="inline-block px-3 py-1 rounded-lg bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-900 font-bold text-xs border border-emerald-200">{recipe.dietType}</span>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
                  <div className="sm:col-span-1 rounded-xl overflow-hidden aspect-4/3 bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 relative shadow-2xs">
                    <img src={recipe.imageUrl || 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1519708227418-c8fd9a32b7a2?auto=format&fit=crop&w=800&q=80'} alt={recipe.name} className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                  </div>

                  <div className="sm:col-span-2 space-y-3 flex flex-col justify-between">
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-center text-xs">
                      {[
                        { label: 'Hazırlık', val: `${recipe.prepTimeMinutes} Dk` },
                        { label: 'Porsiyon', val: `${recipe.servings || 1} Kişilik` },
                        { label: 'Pratiklik', val: recipe.practicality }
                      ].map(box => (
                        <div key={box.label} className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200">
                          <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-medium block">{box.label}</span>
                          <span className="font-bold text-slate-900">{box.val}</span>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>

                    <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-amber-900 text-white text-xs flex items-center justify-between">
                      <span className="font-semibold text-amber-200">Değişim Profili:</span>
                      <span className="font-mono font-bold text-sm">{recipe.exchangeSummary || 'Serbest'}</span>
                    </div>

                    {recipe.totalNutrition && (
                      <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-1.5 text-center text-[11px] font-mono bg-stone-50 p-2 rounded-xl border border-stone-200">
                        {[
                          { label: 'Kalori', val: `${recipe.totalNutrition.calories} kcal` },
                          { label: 'Prot', val: `${recipe.totalNutrition.protein}g` },
                          { label: 'Karb', val: `${recipe.totalNutrition.carbs}g` },
                          { label: 'Yağ', val: `${recipe.totalNutrition.fat}g` }
                        ].map(m => (
                          <div key={m.label}><span className="block text-[9px] text-slate-400 uppercase">{m.label}</span><span className="font-bold text-slate-900">{m.val}</span></div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-6 pt-2">
                  <div className="space-y-3">
                    <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 border-b pb-1.5 flex items-center justify-between">
                      <span>Malzemeler</span><span className="text-xs font-normal text-slate-500">({unifiedIngs.length} İçerik)</span>
                    </h3>
                    <ul className="space-y-1.5 text-xs text-slate-800">
                      {unifiedIngs.map((ing, i) => (
                        <li key={i} className="flex items-start justify-between p-1.5 rounded hover:bg-slate-50">
                          <span className="font-semibold text-slate-900">{ing.foodName}{ing.preparationNote && <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-normal ml-1">({ing.preparationNote})</span>}</span>
                          <span className="font-mono font-bold text-slate-700">{ing.amountDisplay || `${ing.amount} ${ing.unit}`}</span>
                        </li>
                      ))}
                    </ul>
                  </div>

                  <div className="space-y-3">
                    <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 border-b pb-1.5">Hazırlanışı</h3>
                    <div className="space-y-2.5 text-xs text-slate-700">
                      {recipe.steps?.length ? recipe.steps.map(step => (
                        <div key={step.stepNumber} className="flex gap-2.5">
                          <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-slate-900 text-white text-[10px] font-bold flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">{step.stepNumber}</span>
                          <p className="leading-relaxed">{step.instruction}</p>
                        </div>
                      )) : <p className="italic text-slate-400">{recipe.instructions || 'Hazırlık adımları belirtilmemiştir.'}</p>}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {recipe.clinicalNote && (
                  <div className="mt-4 p-3 rounded-xl bg-amber-50/80 border border-amber-200/90 text-xs text-amber-950">
                    <span className="font-bold text-amber-900 block mb-0.5">Diyetisyen Klinik Tavsiyesi:</span>
                    <p className="leading-relaxed">{recipe.clinicalNote}</p>
                  </div>
                )}

                <div className="pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 flex justify-between items-center text-[10px] text-slate-400">
                  <span>{booklet.title} · {booklet.authorName}</span><span>Sayfa #{idx + 1}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

// [06/41] DOSYA: src/components/booklets/BookletsModal.tsx (241 satır)
================================================================================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  X, Plus, BookOpen, Printer, Trash2, Edit2, FolderPlus, Check, ArrowLeft, AlertTriangle
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useNutrition } from '../../context/NutritionContext';
import { RecipeBooklet } from '../../types/nutrition';
import { BookletPrintView } from './BookletPrintView';

interface BookletsModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean; onClose: () => void; preSelectedRecipeIds?: string[];
}

const COVER_THEMES = [
  { id: 'amber', label: 'Kehribar (Amber)', class: 'bg-amber-900 text-white' },
  { id: 'emerald', label: 'Zümrüt Yeşili', class: 'bg-emerald-900 text-white' },
  { id: 'stone', label: 'Koyu Taş / Antrasit', class: 'bg-stone-900 text-white' },
  { id: 'indigo', label: 'Gece Mavisi', class: 'bg-indigo-900 text-white' },
  { id: 'rose', label: 'Gül / Mürdüm', class: 'bg-rose-900 text-white' }
] as const;

export const BookletsModal: React.FC<BookletsModalProps> = ({ isOpen, onClose, preSelectedRecipeIds = [] }) => {
  const { booklets, recipes, saveBooklet, deleteBooklet } = useNutrition();

  const [activePrintBooklet, setActivePrintBooklet] = useState<RecipeBooklet | null>(null);
  const [bookletToDelete, setBookletToDelete] = useState<RecipeBooklet | null>(null);
  const [isCreatingNew, setIsCreatingNew] = useState(preSelectedRecipeIds.length > 0);
  const [editingBookletId, setEditingBookletId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    title: preSelectedRecipeIds.length > 0 ? 'Glutensiz Tatlı Alternatifler - Diyetisyen Hale' : '',
    subtitle: 'Hassas Sindirim & Özel Tarif Seçkisi',
    authorName: 'Dyt. Zeynep Hale Kocaoğlu',
    description: 'Diyetisyen tarafından özenle kurgulanmış porsiyonlu tarif kitapçığı.',
    coverTheme: 'amber' as 'amber' | 'emerald' | 'stone' | 'indigo' | 'rose',
    selectedRecipeIds: preSelectedRecipeIds,
    notes: ''
  });

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleOpenCreateForm = () => {
    setFormData({
      title: 'Glutensiz Tatlı Alternatifler - Diyetisyen Hale',
      subtitle: 'Hassas Sindirim & Özel Tarif Seçkisi',
      authorName: 'Dyt. Zeynep Hale Kocaoğlu',
      description: 'Diyetisyen tarafından özenle kurgulanmış porsiyonlu tarif kitapçığı.',
      coverTheme: 'amber',
      selectedRecipeIds: preSelectedRecipeIds.length > 0 ? preSelectedRecipeIds : [],
      notes: ''
    });
    setEditingBookletId(null);
    setIsCreatingNew(true);
  };

  const handleToggleRecipeInForm = (recipeId: string) => {
    setFormData(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      selectedRecipeIds: prev.selectedRecipeIds.includes(recipeId) ? prev.selectedRecipeIds.filter(id => id !== recipeId) : [...prev.selectedRecipeIds, recipeId]
    }));
  };

  const handleSaveForm = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!formData.title.trim()) { alert('Lütfen kitapçık başlığı giriniz.'); return; }
    saveBooklet({
      id: editingBookletId || undefined, title: formData.title.trim(), subtitle: formData.subtitle.trim(),
      authorName: formData.authorName.trim() || 'Dyt. Zeynep Hale Kocaoğlu', description: formData.description.trim(),
      coverTheme: formData.coverTheme, recipeIds: formData.selectedRecipeIds, notes: formData.notes.trim()
    });
    setIsCreatingNew(false);
    setEditingBookletId(null);
  };

  const handleEditBooklet = (b: RecipeBooklet) => {
    setFormData({
      title: b.title, subtitle: b.subtitle || '', authorName: b.authorName || 'Dyt. Zeynep Hale Kocaoğlu',
      description: b.description || '', coverTheme: b.coverTheme || 'amber', selectedRecipeIds: b.recipeIds || [], notes: b.notes || ''
    });
    setEditingBookletId(b.id);
    setIsCreatingNew(true);
  };

  return (
    <>
      <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 w-full max-w-4xl max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
          <div className="px-6 py-4 bg-slate-900 text-white flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center border border-amber-500/30"><BookOpen className="w-5 h-5" /></div>
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">Kitapçıklar</h2>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">Seçili tarifleri gruplayın ve yazdırılabilir profesyonel kitapçıklar oluşturun</p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => { if (isCreatingNew) { setIsCreatingNew(false); setEditingBookletId(null); } else onClose(); }}
                className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer border border-slate-700"
              >
                <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" /><span>Geri</span>
              </button>
              {!isCreatingNew && (
                <button type="button" onClick={handleOpenCreateForm} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-1.5 bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-400 text-amber-950 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-all cursor-pointer shadow-2xs">
                  <Plus className="w-4 h-4" /><span>+ Yeni Kitapçık Oluştur</span>
                </button>
              )}
              <button type="button" onClick={onClose} className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-800 transition-colors cursor-pointer"><X className="w-5 h-5" /></button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="p-6 overflow-y-auto flex-1 space-y-6">
            {isCreatingNew ? (
              <form onSubmit={handleSaveForm} className="space-y-5 bg-slate-50 p-5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-slate-200">
                  <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                    <FolderPlus className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-800" /><span>{editingBookletId ? 'Kitapçığı Düzenle' : 'Yeni Kitapçık Oluştur'}</span>
                  </h3>
                  <button type="button" onClick={() => { setIsCreatingNew(false); setEditingBookletId(null); }} className="text-xs text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 font-semibold cursor-pointer">Listeye Dön</button>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 text-xs">
                  <div className="sm:col-span-2">
                    <label className="block font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-1">Kitapçık Başlığı *</label>
                    <input type="text" required value={formData.title} onChange={e => setFormData(p => ({ ...p, title: e.target.value }))} placeholder="Örn: Glutensiz Tatlı Alternatifler - Diyetisyen Hale" className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white rounded-lg border border-slate-300 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-800" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <label className="block font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-1">Alt Başlık / Kapsam</label>
                    <input type="text" value={formData.subtitle} onChange={e => setFormData(p => ({ ...p, subtitle: e.target.value }))} placeholder="Örn: Hassas Sindirim & Özel Tarif Seçkisi" className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white rounded-lg border border-slate-300 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-800" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <label className="block font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-1">Hazırlayan Diyetisyen</label>
                    <input type="text" value={formData.authorName} onChange={e => setFormData(p => ({ ...p, authorName: e.target.value }))} placeholder="Dyt. Zeynep Hale Kocaoğlu" className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white rounded-lg border border-slate-300 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-800" />
                  </div>
                  <div className="sm:col-span-2">
                    <label className="block font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-1.5">Kapak Teması / Renk Stili</label>
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                      {COVER_THEMES.map(theme => (
                        <button key={theme.id} type="button" onClick={() => setFormData(p => ({ ...p, coverTheme: theme.id }))} className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold border transition-all cursor-pointer ${theme.class} ${formData.coverTheme === theme.id ? 'ring-2 ring-offset-1 ring-amber-800 scale-105' : 'opacity-70 hover:opacity-100'}`}>
                          {theme.label}
                        </button>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="sm:col-span-2">
                    <label className="block font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-1">Kitapçık Açıklaması / Ön Söz</label>
                    <textarea rows={2} value={formData.description} onChange={e => setFormData(p => ({ ...p, description: e.target.value }))} placeholder="Danışanlara yönelik kısa açıklama..." className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white rounded-lg border border-slate-300 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-800" />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="space-y-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-200">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-900">Kitapçığa Dahil Edilecek Reçeteler ({formData.selectedRecipeIds.length} Seçili)</label>
                    <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">Kütüphanedeki tüm tarifler listelenmiştir</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2 max-h-56 overflow-y-auto p-2 bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
                    {recipes.map(recipe => {
                      const isSelected = formData.selectedRecipeIds.includes(recipe.id);
                      return (
                        <div key={recipe.id} onClick={() => handleToggleRecipeInForm(recipe.id)} className={`p-2.5 rounded-lg border flex items-center justify-between gap-2 cursor-pointer transition-all ${isSelected ? 'bg-amber-50/80 border-amber-300 text-amber-950 font-semibold' : 'bg-stone-50/50 border-slate-200 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100'}`}>
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 overflow-hidden">
                            <span className={`w-4 h-4 rounded flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] shrink-0 border ${isSelected ? 'bg-amber-900 text-white border-amber-900' : 'border-slate-300 bg-white'}`}>
                              {isSelected && <Check className="w-3 h-3" />}
                            </span>
                            <span className="text-xs truncate">{recipe.name}</span>
                          </div>
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-bold text-slate-500 shrink-0">{recipe.dietType}</span>
                        </div>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-200">
                  {editingBookletId ? (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => { const target = booklets.find(b => b.id === editingBookletId); if (target) setBookletToDelete(target); }}
                      className="px-3.5 py-2 bg-rose-50 hover:bg-rose-100 text-rose-700 border border-rose-200 font-semibold rounded-lg text-xs transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /><span>Kitapçığı Sil</span>
                    </button>
                  ) : <div />}
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <button type="button" onClick={() => { setIsCreatingNew(false); setEditingBookletId(null); }} className="px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 font-semibold rounded-lg text-xs transition-colors cursor-pointer">İptal</button>
                    <button type="submit" className="px-5 py-2 bg-amber-900 hover:bg-amber-950 text-white font-bold rounded-lg text-xs shadow-md transition-all cursor-pointer">Kitapçığı Kaydet</button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </form>
            ) : (
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                {booklets.map(b => (
                  <div key={b.id} className="bg-stone-50/70 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/90 p-5 space-y-4 flex flex-col justify-between hover:border-slate-300 transition-all shadow-2xs">
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                        <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-mono font-bold bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 border border-amber-200">{b.recipeIds?.length || 0} Tarif İçerir</span>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                          <button type="button" onClick={() => handleEditBooklet(b)} className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-800 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-200 transition-colors cursor-pointer" title="Düzenle"><Edit2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /></button>
                          <button type="button" onClick={() => setBookletToDelete(b)} className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-600 rounded-lg hover:bg-rose-50 transition-colors cursor-pointer" title="Sil"><Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /></button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <h3 className="text-base font-bold font-serif text-slate-900 leading-snug">{b.title}</h3>
                      {b.subtitle && <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">{b.subtitle}</p>}
                      {b.description && <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 line-clamp-2 leading-relaxed pt-1">{b.description}</p>}
                    </div>

                    <div className="pt-3 border-t border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between">
                      <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-amber-950">{b.authorName || 'Dyt. Zeynep Hale Kocaoğlu'}</span>
                      <button type="button" onClick={() => setActivePrintBooklet(b)} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-1.5 bg-slate-900 hover:bg-amber-900 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all cursor-pointer shadow-2xs">
                        <Printer className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" /><span>Kitapçığı İncele & Yazdır</span>
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {activePrintBooklet && <BookletPrintView booklet={activePrintBooklet} recipes={recipes} onClose={() => setActivePrintBooklet(null)} />}

      {bookletToDelete && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[120] bg-slate-900/80 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 animate-in fade-in">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 w-full max-w-md overflow-hidden text-slate-900 animate-in zoom-in-95 p-6 text-center space-y-4">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-rose-100 text-rose-600 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto"><AlertTriangle className="w-6 h-6 text-rose-600" /></div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">Silmek İstediğinize Emin Misiniz?</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-2 leading-relaxed"><span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">"{bookletToDelete.title}"</span> kitapçığı ve içeriğindeki gruplandırma silinecektir. Bu işlem geri alınamaz.</p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 pt-2">
              <button type="button" onClick={() => setBookletToDelete(null)} className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-100 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 transition-colors cursor-pointer flex-1">Vazgeç</button>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => { deleteBooklet(bookletToDelete.id); setBookletToDelete(null); setIsCreatingNew(false); setEditingBookletId(null); }} className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 text-white text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer flex-1 shadow-2xs">Evet, Kalıcı Olarak Sil</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
};

import React from 'react';

export type ChiaPose = 'planning' | 'balanced' | 'suggesting' | 'habits' | 'waving' | 'icon';

interface ChiaMascotProps {
  pose?: ChiaPose; size?: number | string; className?: string; showSpeechBubble?: boolean; speechText?: string;
}

const RobotHeadAndVisor: React.FC<{ closedEyes?: boolean; tilted?: boolean }> = ({ closedEyes = false, tilted = false }) => {
  const head = (
    <>
      <path d="M 45,85 C 45,45 68,32 100,32 C 132,32 155,45 155,85 C 155,122 132,135 100,135 C 68,135 45,122 45,85 Z" fill="#FAFAFA" stroke="#E2E8E0" strokeWidth="2" />
      <rect x="34" y="72" width="13" height="26" rx="6.5" fill="#4B6543" />
      <rect x="153" y="72" width="13" height="26" rx="6.5" fill="#4B6543" />
      <path d="M 55,85 C 55,58 72,48 100,48 C 128,48 145,58 145,85 C 145,112 128,122 100,122 C 72,122 55,112 55,85 Z" fill="#232E21" />
      {closedEyes ? (
        <>
          <path d="M 68,80 C 75,88 85,88 92,80" stroke="#FFFFFF" strokeWidth="4.5" strokeLinecap="round" fill="none" />
          <path d="M 108,80 C 115,88 125,88 132,80" stroke="#FFFFFF" strokeWidth="4.5" strokeLinecap="round" fill="none" />
        </>
      ) : (
        <>
          <path d="M 68,82 C 73,70 83,70 88,82" stroke="#FFFFFF" strokeWidth="4.5" strokeLinecap="round" />
          <path d="M 112,82 C 117,70 127,70 132,82" stroke="#FFFFFF" strokeWidth="4.5" strokeLinecap="round" />
          <ellipse cx="66" cy="94" rx="4" ry="2.5" fill="#698C5F" />
          <ellipse cx="134" cy="94" rx="4" ry="2.5" fill="#698C5F" />
        </>
      )}
    </>
  );
  return (
    <>
      <g transform="translate(100, 32)"><path d="M -16,-18 C -28,-36 0,-36 0,-16 C 0,-36 28,-36 16,-18 Z" fill="#4B6543" /></g>
      {tilted ? <g transform="rotate(-5, 100, 85)">{head}</g> : head}
    </>
  );
};

export const ChiaMascot: React.FC<ChiaMascotProps> = ({ pose = 'waving', size = 64, className = '', showSpeechBubble = false, speechText }) => {
  const pixelSize = typeof size === 'number' ? `${size}px` : size;
  return (
    <div className={`relative inline-flex items-center justify-center ${className}`}>
      <div style={{ width: pixelSize, height: pixelSize }}>
        {pose === 'icon' ? (
          <svg viewBox="0 0 200 200" className="w-full h-full drop-shadow-sm" fill="none">
            <rect width="200" height="200" rx="48" fill="#58724F" />
            <path d="M 50,110 C 50,70 70,55 100,55 C 130,55 150,70 150,110 C 150,145 130,160 100,160 C 70,160 50,145 50,110 Z" fill="#FFFFFF" />
            <rect x="38" y="95" width="14" height="30" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" />
            <rect x="148" y="95" width="14" height="30" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" />
            <path d="M 60,110 C 60,82 75,72 100,72 C 125,72 140,82 140,110 C 140,138 125,148 100,148 C 75,148 60,138 60,110 Z" fill="#202A1D" />
            <path d="M 72,108 C 77,96 87,96 92,108" stroke="#FFFFFF" strokeWidth="5" strokeLinecap="round" />
            <path d="M 108,108 C 113,96 123,96 128,108" stroke="#FFFFFF" strokeWidth="5" strokeLinecap="round" />
            <ellipse cx="70" cy="120" rx="5" ry="3" fill="#7FA174" opacity="0.8" />
            <ellipse cx="130" cy="120" rx="5" ry="3" fill="#7FA174" opacity="0.8" />
            <g transform="translate(100, 55)">
              <path d="M 0,0 Q -2,-12 0,-18" stroke="#FFFFFF" strokeWidth="4" strokeLinecap="round" />
              <path d="M 0,-18 C -15,-32 -28,-18 0,-18 Z" fill="#FFFFFF" />
              <path d="M 0,-18 C 15,-32 28,-18 0,-18 Z" fill="#FFFFFF" />
            </g>
          </svg>
        ) : (
          <svg viewBox="0 0 200 240" className="w-full h-full drop-shadow-md" fill="none">
            <RobotHeadAndVisor closedEyes={pose === 'habits'} tilted={pose === 'suggesting'} />
            <path d="M 70,132 L 130,132 L 138,210 C 138,220 125,225 100,225 C 75,225 62,220 62,210 Z" fill="#F4F4F0" />
            <path d="M 72,142 L 128,142 L 133,212 C 133,218 120,222 100,222 C 80,222 67,218 67,212 Z" fill="#58724F" />
            {(pose === 'planning' || pose === 'waving') && <path d="M 100,165 Q 92,152 100,148 Q 108,152 100,165 Z" fill="#E8F0E5" />}
            {pose === 'planning' && (
              <>
                <g transform="translate(108, 140) rotate(-10)">
                  <rect width="55" height="42" rx="6" fill="#889882" stroke="#4B6543" strokeWidth="2" />
                  <rect x="4" y="4" width="47" height="34" rx="3" fill="#EAF0E8" />
                  <line x1="10" y1="12" x2="35" y2="12" stroke="#58724F" strokeWidth="2.5" strokeLinecap="round" />
                  <line x1="10" y1="20" x2="28" y2="20" stroke="#889882" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" />
                  <line x1="10" y1="28" x2="38" y2="28" stroke="#E07A5F" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" />
                </g>
                <ellipse cx="100" cy="170" rx="8" ry="6" fill="#FAFAFA" />
                <path d="M 152,48 L 162,40 M 160,60 L 172,58 M 156,72 L 168,76" stroke="#58724F" strokeWidth="3" strokeLinecap="round" />
              </>
            )}
            {pose === 'balanced' && (
              <>
                <g transform="translate(48, 145)">
                  <circle cx="20" cy="10" r="14" fill="#4E8752" /><circle cx="38" cy="8" r="16" fill="#386A3C" /><circle cx="58" cy="12" r="13" fill="#65A36A" />
                  <circle cx="28" cy="5" r="5" fill="#E07A5F" /><circle cx="48" cy="3" r="4" fill="#F4A261" /><circle cx="36" cy="14" r="4" fill="#E07A5F" />
                  <path d="M 5,16 C 5,16 10,40 40,40 C 70,40 75,16 75,16 Z" fill="#EAE8E0" stroke="#D0CDBF" strokeWidth="2" />
                </g>
                <path d="M 148,120 C 148,114 154,110 159,114 C 164,110 170,114 170,120 C 170,128 159,134 159,134 C 159,134 148,128 148,120 Z" fill="#E07A5F" />
              </>
            )}
            {pose === 'suggesting' && (
              <>
                <path d="M 115,180 C 120,160 125,130 118,122" stroke="#FAFAFA" strokeWidth="12" strokeLinecap="round" />
                <circle cx="118" cy="122" r="7" fill="#FAFAFA" />
                <text x="160" y="70" fill="#4B6543" fontSize="36" fontWeight="bold" fontFamily="sans-serif">?</text>
              </>
            )}
            {pose === 'habits' && (
              <g transform="translate(82, 160)">
                <rect width="36" height="30" rx="6" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#889882" strokeWidth="2" />
                <path d="M 36,8 C 44,8 44,22 36,22" stroke="#889882" strokeWidth="2" fill="none" />
                <path d="M 18,12 Q 13,20 18,22 Q 23,20 18,12 Z" fill="#58724F" />
                <path d="M 10,-8 Q 14,-14 10,-20 M 26,-8 Q 22,-14 26,-20" stroke="#B2C2B0" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" fill="none" />
              </g>
            )}
            {pose === 'waving' && (
              <>
                <g transform="translate(145, 120) rotate(-25)">
                  <path d="M 0,0 C 15,-10 25,-25 35,-35" stroke="#FAFAFA" strokeWidth="12" strokeLinecap="round" />
                  <circle cx="35" cy="-35" r="8" fill="#FAFAFA" />
                </g>
                <path d="M 180,75 C 185,82 185,92 180,98 M 190,70 C 197,80 197,95 190,105" stroke="#58724F" strokeWidth="2.5" strokeLinecap="round" fill="none" />
              </>
            )}
          </svg>
        )}
      </div>
      {showSpeechBubble && speechText && (
        <div className="absolute left-full ml-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 bg-white px-3 py-2 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-200/80 shadow-xs text-xs font-medium text-slate-800 whitespace-nowrap z-10 animate-fade-in">
          <div className="absolute -left-1.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-3 h-3 bg-white border-b border-l border-emerald-200/80 rotate-45" />
          {speechText}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export const ChiaLogo: React.FC<{ size?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg'; className?: string }> = ({ size = 'md', className = '' }) => {
  const iconSizes = { sm: 32, md: 40, lg: 48 };
  const textSizes = { sm: 'text-lg', md: 'text-2xl', lg: 'text-3xl' };
  return (
    <div className={`flex items-center gap-2.5 ${className}`}>
      <div className="shrink-0 transition-transform hover:scale-105 cursor-pointer"><ChiaMascot pose="icon" size={iconSizes[size]} /></div>
      <div className={`font-bold tracking-tight text-[#3B5434] font-sans flex items-center leading-none ${textSizes[size]}`}>chia</div>
    </div>
  );
};

export const ChiaAssistantBanner: React.FC<{
  pose?: ChiaPose; title: string; description: string; badgeText?: string; actionButton?: React.ReactNode; className?: string;
}> = ({ pose = 'balanced', title, description, badgeText = 'Chia Rehberlik Ediyor', actionButton, className = '' }) => (
  <div className={`p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-[#F2F6F0] via-[#FAF8F3] to-[#EAF0E8] border border-[#C9D8C3] shadow-2xs flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-start justify-between gap-4 ${className}`}>
    <div className="flex items-start sm:items-center gap-3.5">
      <div className="shrink-0 p-1 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-[#C9D8C3] shadow-2xs"><ChiaMascot pose={pose} size={56} /></div>
      <div className="space-y-1">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#3B5434] bg-[#E3ECE0] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-[#C9D8C3]">{badgeText}</span>
        </div>
        <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-[#1F2B1D]">{title}</h3>
        <p className="text-xs text-[#4A5D46] max-w-2xl leading-relaxed">{description}</p>
      </div>
    </div>
    {actionButton && <div className="shrink-0 self-start md:self-start flex items-center justify-end">{actionButton}</div>}
  </div>
);

// [08/41] DOSYA: src/components/exchanges/ExchangeRulesView.tsx (309 satır)

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  Layers, Zap, Search, Plus, Edit, Trash2, X, Save, Filter, Info, RotateCcw, AlertTriangle
} from 'lucide-react';
import { ExchangeFoodItem, INITIAL_EXCHANGE_FOODS } from '../../data/mockExchangeFoods';
import { ChiaAssistantBanner } from '../common/ChiaMascot';

const LOCAL_STORAGE_KEY = 'nutriplan_exchange_foods_v1';

const GROUPS = [
  'Tümü', 'Süt & Yoğurt Grubu', 'Et, Peynir, Yumurta Grubu', 'Ekmek, Tahıl, Kurubaklagil Grubu',
  'Meyve Grubu', 'Sebze Grubu', 'Yağ Grubu', 'Yağlı Tohumlar & Kuruyemiş Grubu'
];

const EXCHANGE_STANDARDS = [
  { group: 'Et / Protein Değişimi', protein: 6, carbs: 0, fat: 5, calories: 69 },
  { group: 'Ekmek / Tahıl Değişimi', protein: 2, carbs: 15, fat: 0, calories: 68 },
  { group: 'Süt / Yoğurt Değişimi', protein: 6, carbs: 9, fat: 6, calories: 114 },
  { group: 'Yağ Değişimi', protein: 0, carbs: 0, fat: 5, calories: 45 },
  { group: 'Sebze Değişimi', protein: 2, carbs: 6, fat: 0, calories: 32 },
  { group: 'Meyve Değişimi', protein: 0, carbs: 15, fat: 0, calories: 60 }
];

export const ExchangeRulesView: React.FC = () => {
  const [showRulesInfo, setShowRulesInfo] = useState(false);

  const [exchangeFoods, setExchangeFoods] = useState<ExchangeFoodItem[]>(() => {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(LOCAL_STORAGE_KEY);
      if (saved) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(saved);
        if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0) return parsed;
      }
    } catch {}
    return INITIAL_EXCHANGE_FOODS;
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    try { localStorage.setItem(LOCAL_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(exchangeFoods)); } catch {}
  }, [exchangeFoods]);

  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [selectedGroup, setSelectedGroup] = useState<string>('Tümü');
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingItem, setEditingItem] = useState<ExchangeFoodItem | null>(null);
  const [itemToDelete, setItemToDelete] = useState<{ id: string; name: string } | null>(null);
  const [showResetConfirm, setShowResetConfirm] = useState(false);

  const [formData, setFormData] = useState<Omit<ExchangeFoodItem, 'id'>>({
    name: '', group: 'Et, Peynir, Yumurta Grubu', subGroup: 'Peynirler',
    portion: '1 porsiyon', amountNet: '30 g', stateNote: '30 g', equivalents: '', clinicalNote: ''
  });

  const filteredFoods = exchangeFoods.filter(item => {
    const matchesGroup = selectedGroup === 'Tümü' || item.group === selectedGroup;
    const query = searchQuery.toLowerCase().trim();
    const matchesQuery =
      !query || item.name.toLowerCase().includes(query) || item.group.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
      item.subGroup.toLowerCase().includes(query) || item.equivalents.toLowerCase().includes(query) || item.clinicalNote.toLowerCase().includes(query);
    return matchesGroup && matchesQuery;
  });

  const handleOpenAddModal = () => {
    setEditingItem(null);
    setFormData({
      name: '', group: selectedGroup !== 'Tümü' ? selectedGroup : 'Et, Peynir, Yumurta Grubu',
      subGroup: 'Kırmızı Et (Yağsız)', portion: '1 porsiyon', amountNet: '30 g', stateNote: '30 g', equivalents: '', clinicalNote: ''
    });
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleOpenEditModal = (item: ExchangeFoodItem) => {
    setEditingItem(item);
    setFormData({
      name: item.name, group: item.group, subGroup: item.subGroup, portion: item.portion,
      amountNet: item.amountNet, stateNote: item.stateNote, equivalents: item.equivalents, clinicalNote: item.clinicalNote
    });
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleSaveItem = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!formData.name.trim()) return;
    if (editingItem) setExchangeFoods(prev => prev.map(f => (f.id === editingItem.id ? { ...f, ...formData } : f)));
    else setExchangeFoods(prev => [{ id: `ex-custom-${Date.now()}`, ...formData }, ...prev]);
    setIsModalOpen(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 pb-3 border-b border-slate-200">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold font-serif text-slate-900 tracking-tight flex items-center gap-2">
            <Layers className="w-6 h-6 text-amber-900" /><span>Klinik Değişim Sistemi</span>
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1 max-w-2xl">Süt, et, tahıl, meyve, sebze ve yağ gruplarının ölçü, gramaj, klinik eşdeğer alternatifleri ve fonksiyon notları.</p>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button" onClick={() => setShowRulesInfo(!showRulesInfo)}
          className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border text-xs font-semibold transition-all cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
            showRulesInfo ? 'bg-[#3F5338] border-[#3F5338] text-white shadow-2xs' : 'border-[#C9D8C3] bg-[#FAF8F3] text-[#2F432A] hover:bg-[#E3ECE0]'
          }`}
        >
          <Info className="w-4 h-4" /><span>Şablon Dağılım & Değişim Kuralları Bilgisi</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      <ChiaAssistantBanner
        pose="suggesting" badgeText="Chia Değişim Asistanı" title="Sana Özel Öneriler Sunuyor"
        description="Besinlerin porsiyon, gramaj, kalori ve makro değerlerini inceleyin. Hastalık ve diyet tipine göre alternatif değişim eşdeğerlerini kullanarak danışanlarınıza tam uyumlu listeler hazırlayabilirsiniz."
      />

      {showRulesInfo && (
        <div className="p-5 bg-amber-50/60 rounded-2xl border border-amber-200 space-y-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-amber-950 flex items-center gap-2"><Zap className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-800" />Şablon Dağılım & Değişim Kuralları Mimarisi</h3>
            <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowRulesInfo(false)} className="text-xs text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-900 font-semibold cursor-pointer">Kapat ✕</button>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-amber-950/80 leading-relaxed">
            NutriPlan mimarisinde tarifler sabit değişimlerle kısıtlanmaz; seçilen klinik arketip ve diyet modellerine göre dinamik olarak yorumlanır. Menü motoru belirlenen protein, karbonhidrat ve yağ sınır kurallarına tam uyum sağlar.
          </p>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
            {EXCHANGE_STANDARDS.map(item => (
              <div key={item.group} className="p-3 bg-white rounded-xl border border-amber-200/60 space-y-2">
                <div className="font-bold text-xs text-slate-900">{item.group}</div>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-1 text-center font-mono text-[11px] bg-slate-50 p-1.5 rounded border border-slate-100">
                  <div><span className="text-[9px] text-slate-400 block">Kcal</span><strong className="text-slate-900">{item.calories}</strong></div>
                  <div><span className="text-[9px] text-slate-400 block">P</span><strong className="text-slate-700">{item.protein}g</strong></div>
                  <div><span className="text-[9px] text-slate-400 block">K</span><strong className="text-slate-700">{item.carbs}g</strong></div>
                  <div><span className="text-[9px] text-slate-400 block">Y</span><strong className="text-slate-700">{item.fat}g</strong></div>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="space-y-5">
        <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-2xs space-y-3">
          <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div className="relative flex-1">
              <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5" />
              <input type="text" value={searchQuery} onChange={e => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)} placeholder="Yiyecek adı, grup, klinik alternatif veya fonksiyon ara..." className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-800" />
              {searchQuery && <button onClick={() => setSearchQuery('')} className="absolute right-3 top-2.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700"><X className="w-4 h-4" /></button>}
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
              <button onClick={handleOpenAddModal} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-2 bg-amber-900 hover:bg-amber-800 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold shadow-2xs transition-colors cursor-pointer">
                <Plus className="w-4 h-4" /><span>Yeni Değişim Besini Ekle</span>
              </button>
              <button onClick={() => setShowResetConfirm(true)} title="Listeyi Varsayılana Sıfırla" className="p-2 border border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium transition-colors cursor-pointer">
                <RotateCcw className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto pb-1 pt-1 text-xs">
            <span className="text-slate-400 font-semibold text-[11px] shrink-0 mr-1 flex items-center gap-1"><Filter className="w-3 h-3" /> Grup:</span>
            {GROUPS.map(grp => (
              <button key={grp} onClick={() => setSelectedGroup(grp)} className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-lg border text-xs font-medium transition-all whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer ${selectedGroup === grp ? 'bg-amber-900 border-amber-900 text-white font-semibold' : 'bg-slate-50 border-slate-200 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100'}`}>
                {grp}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-slate-500 px-1">
          <span>Listelenen Besin Sayısı: <strong className="text-slate-900 font-bold">{filteredFoods.length}</strong> / {exchangeFoods.length}</span>
          {selectedGroup !== 'Tümü' && <span className="text-amber-900 font-semibold bg-amber-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-amber-200">Filtre: {selectedGroup}</span>}
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-2xs overflow-hidden">
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-xs">
              <thead>
                <tr className="bg-slate-100/80 border-b border-slate-200 text-slate-700 font-bold">
                  <th className="py-3 px-3.5">Yiyecek Adı</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-3">Grup & Alt Grup</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-3">Ortalama Ölçü (1 Değişim)</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-3">Miktar / Net (g)</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-3">Çiğ/Pişmiş veya Brüt/Net</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-3 min-w-[180px]">Klinik Eşdeğer Alternatifler</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-3 min-w-[200px]">Klinik Not & Fonksiyon</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-3 text-right">İşlem</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 text-slate-800 font-medium">
                {filteredFoods.length === 0 ? (
                  <tr><td colSpan={8} className="py-12 text-center text-slate-400">Arama veya filtre kriterlerinize uygun değişim besini bulunamadı.</td></tr>
                ) : (
                  filteredFoods.map(item => (
                    <tr key={item.id} className="hover:bg-amber-50/40 transition-colors group">
                      <td className="py-2.5 px-3.5 font-bold text-slate-900">{item.name}</td>
                      <td className="py-2.5 px-3"><div className="font-semibold text-slate-900">{item.group}</div><div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-normal">{item.subGroup}</div></td>
                      <td className="py-2.5 px-3 font-semibold text-amber-950 bg-amber-50/30">{item.portion}</td>
                      <td className="py-2.5 px-3 font-mono font-bold text-slate-900">{item.amountNet}</td>
                      <td className="py-2.5 px-3 text-slate-600 font-mono text-[11px]">{item.stateNote}</td>
                      <td className="py-2.5 px-3 text-slate-700 leading-snug">{item.equivalents || '-'}</td>
                      <td className="py-2.5 px-3 text-slate-600 italic leading-snug">{item.clinicalNote || '-'}</td>
                      <td className="py-2.5 px-3 text-right whitespace-nowrap">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1">
                          <button onClick={() => handleOpenEditModal(item)} className="px-2 py-1 text-slate-600 hover:text-amber-900 hover:bg-amber-100/60 rounded font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1">
                            <Edit className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /><span>Düzenle</span>
                          </button>
                          <button onClick={() => setItemToDelete({ id: item.id, name: item.name })} className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-50 rounded transition-colors cursor-pointer" title="Sil">
                            <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))
                )}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {isModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 w-full max-w-xl overflow-hidden animate-in fade-in zoom-in duration-150">
            <div className="px-5 py-3.5 border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 flex items-center justify-between">
              <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">{editingItem ? 'Değişim Besinini Düzenle' : 'Yeni Değişim Besini Ekle'}</h3>
              <button onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(false)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 p-1 rounded-lg"><X className="w-4 h-4" /></button>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleSaveItem} className="p-5 space-y-4 text-xs">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div className="sm:col-span-2">
                  <label className="block font-semibold text-slate-900 mb-1">Yiyecek Adı *</label>
                  <input type="text" required value={formData.name} onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, name: e.target.value })} placeholder="Örn: Ev Yoğurdu" className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 font-medium text-slate-900" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block font-semibold text-slate-900 mb-1">Grup</label>
                  <select value={formData.group} onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, group: e.target.value })} className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 font-medium text-slate-900">
                    {GROUPS.filter(g => g !== 'Tümü').map(g => <option key={g} value={g}>{g}</option>)}
                  </select>
                </div>
                {[
                  { key: 'subGroup' as const, label: 'Alt Grup', ph: 'Örn: Süt & Yoğurt' },
                  { key: 'portion' as const, label: 'Ortalama Ölçü (1 Değişim)', ph: 'Örn: 1 su bardağı' },
                  { key: 'amountNet' as const, label: 'Miktar / Net (g)', ph: 'Örn: 200 g' }
                ].map(f => (
                  <div key={f.key}>
                    <label className="block font-semibold text-slate-900 mb-1">{f.label}</label>
                    <input type="text" value={formData[f.key]} onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, [f.key]: e.target.value })} placeholder={f.ph} className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 font-medium text-slate-900" />
                  </div>
                ))}
                {[
                  { key: 'stateNote' as const, label: 'Çiğ/Pişmiş veya Brüt/Net Durumu', ph: 'Örn: 30g Çiğ / 20g Pişmiş' },
                  { key: 'equivalents' as const, label: 'Klinik Eşdeğer Alternatifler', ph: 'Örn: 200g Ev Yoğurdu, 200g Kefir' }
                ].map(f => (
                  <div key={f.key} className="sm:col-span-2">
                    <label className="block font-semibold text-slate-900 mb-1">{f.label}</label>
                    <input type="text" value={formData[f.key]} onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, [f.key]: e.target.value })} placeholder={f.ph} className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 font-medium text-slate-900" />
                  </div>
                ))}
                <div className="sm:col-span-2">
                  <label className="block font-semibold text-slate-900 mb-1">Klinik Not & Fonksiyon</label>
                  <textarea rows={2} value={formData.clinicalNote} onChange={e => setFormData({ ...formData, clinicalNote: e.target.value })} placeholder="Örn: Doğal kalsiyum ve fosfor kaynağı." className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 font-medium text-slate-900" />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
                <button type="button" onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(false)} className="px-4 py-2 border border-slate-200 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded-lg font-semibold cursor-pointer">İptal</button>
                <button type="submit" className="px-4 py-2 bg-amber-900 hover:bg-amber-800 text-white rounded-lg font-semibold cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Save className="w-4 h-4" /><span>{editingItem ? 'Güncelle' : 'Kaydet'}</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {itemToDelete && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/75 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 animate-in fade-in">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 w-full max-w-sm p-6 text-center space-y-4">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-rose-100 text-rose-600 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto"><AlertTriangle className="w-6 h-6" /></div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">Değişim Besinini Sil</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1"><strong className="text-slate-800">"{itemToDelete.name}"</strong> isimli değişim besinini listeden silmek istediğinize emin misiniz?</p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 pt-2">
              <button type="button" onClick={() => setItemToDelete(null)} className="flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-100 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 cursor-pointer">Vazgeç</button>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => { setExchangeFoods(prev => prev.filter(f => f.id !== itemToDelete.id)); setItemToDelete(null); }} className="flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 text-white text-xs font-semibold shadow-2xs cursor-pointer">Evet, Sil</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {showResetConfirm && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/75 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 animate-in fade-in">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 w-full max-w-sm p-6 text-center space-y-4">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto"><RotateCcw className="w-6 h-6" /></div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">Varsayılan Listeye Sıfırla</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">Tüm değişim besinleri orijinal 159 besinlik klinik referans listesine sıfırlansın mı?</p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 pt-2">
              <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowResetConfirm(false)} className="flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-100 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 cursor-pointer">Vazgeç</button>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => { setExchangeFoods(INITIAL_EXCHANGE_FOODS); localStorage.removeItem(LOCAL_STORAGE_KEY); setShowResetConfirm(false); }} className="flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-900 hover:bg-amber-950 text-white text-xs font-semibold shadow-2xs cursor-pointer">Sıfırla</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};
