import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect, useMemo, useCallback } from 'react';
import { Recipe, FoodItem, ClientProfile, RecipeIngredient, RecipeBooklet } from '../types/nutrition';
import { INITIAL_RECIPES } from '../data/mockRecipes';
import { INITIAL_FOODS } from '../data/mockFoods';
import { INITIAL_CLIENTS } from '../data/mockArchetypes';
import { INITIAL_BOOKLETS } from '../data/mockBooklets';
import { turKompMockService, TurKompDatabaseService, TurKompDatabaseStats, IngredientCalculationResult } from '../services/turKompDatabaseService';

export type ActiveModule = 'recipes' | 'foods' | 'exchanges' | 'templates';

export interface RecipeFilters {
  search: string; dietType: string; mealType: string; practicality: string;
  transportability: string; category: string; season: string; status: 'Tümü' | 'Aktif' | 'Taslak';
}

interface NutritionContextType {
  activeModule: ActiveModule; setActiveModule: (mod: ActiveModule) => void;
  recipes: Recipe[];
  recipeMap: ReadonlyMap<string, Recipe>;
  filters: RecipeFilters; setFilters: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<RecipeFilters>>;
  filteredRecipes: Recipe[]; saveRecipe: (recipe: Recipe) => void; deleteRecipe: (id: string) => void;
  booklets: RecipeBooklet[];
  saveBooklet: (bookletData: Omit<RecipeBooklet, 'id' | 'createdAt' | 'updatedAt'> & { id?: string }) => RecipeBooklet;
  deleteBooklet: (id: string) => void; addRecipesToBooklet: (bookletId: string, recipeIdsToAdd: string[]) => void;
  isWizardOpen: boolean; editingRecipe: Recipe | null; openWizard: (recipe?: Recipe) => void; closeWizard: () => void;
  selectedRecipeForDetail: Recipe | null; openDetail: (recipe: Recipe) => void; closeDetail: () => void;
  foods: FoodItem[]; addCustomFood: (food: FoodItem) => void; turKompService: TurKompDatabaseService;
  turKompStats: TurKompDatabaseStats;
  calculateIngredientNutrition: (foodOrId: FoodItem | string, amount: number, unit: string) => IngredientCalculationResult;
  calculateRecipeTotals: (ingredients: RecipeIngredient[]) => ReturnType<typeof turKompMockService.calculateRecipeTotals>;
  recalculateRecipe: (recipe: Recipe) => Recipe;
  clients: ClientProfile[];
  activePlannerClientId: string | null; setActivePlannerClientId: (id: string | null) => void;
  stats: {
    totalRecipes: number; activeRecipes: number; draftRecipes: number; totalFoods: number;
    turKompCount: number; usdaCount: number;
  };
}

const NutritionContext = createContext<NutritionContextType | undefined>(undefined);

const RECIPES_STORAGE_KEY = 'nutriplan_recipes_v3';
const FOODS_STORAGE_KEY = 'nutriplan_foods_v2';
const BOOKLETS_STORAGE_KEY = 'nutriplan_booklets_v1';

function loadStoredArray<T>(key: string, fallback: T[]): T[] {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(key);
    if (!raw) return fallback;
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    return Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0 ? parsed : fallback;
  } catch {
    return fallback;
  }
}

function useDebouncedStorage<T>(key: string, value: T, delay = 300) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      try { localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(value)); } catch {}
    }, delay);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [key, value, delay]);
}

export const NutritionProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const [activeModule, setActiveModule] = useState<ActiveModule>('recipes');
  const [activePlannerClientId, setActivePlannerClientId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [recipes, setRecipes] = useState<Recipe[]>(() => {
    const stored = loadStoredArray(RECIPES_STORAGE_KEY, INITIAL_RECIPES);
    const storedMap = new Map(stored.map(r => [r.id, r]));
    const merged = [...stored];
    INITIAL_RECIPES.forEach(seed => {
      if (!storedMap.has(seed.id)) merged.push(seed);
    });
    return merged.map(r => turKompMockService.recalculateRecipe(r));
  });

  const [foods, setFoods] = useState<FoodItem[]>(() => {
    const stored = loadStoredArray(FOODS_STORAGE_KEY, INITIAL_FOODS);
    const storedMap = new Map(stored.map(f => [f.id, f]));
    const merged = [...stored];
    INITIAL_FOODS.forEach(seed => {
      if (!storedMap.has(seed.id)) merged.push(seed);
    });
    return merged;
  });

  const [booklets, setBooklets] = useState<RecipeBooklet[]>(() => loadStoredArray(BOOKLETS_STORAGE_KEY, INITIAL_BOOKLETS));
  // TODO [ORBIO-INTEGRATION]: Orbio ana danışanlar modülüyle birleştiğinde INITIAL_CLIENTS silinecek ve Orbio state'ine bağlanacaktır.
  const [clients] = useState<ClientProfile[]>(INITIAL_CLIENTS);

  useDebouncedStorage(RECIPES_STORAGE_KEY, recipes);
  useDebouncedStorage(FOODS_STORAGE_KEY, foods);
  useDebouncedStorage(BOOKLETS_STORAGE_KEY, booklets);

  const recipeMap = useMemo(() => new Map(recipes.map(r => [r.id, r])), [recipes]);

  const saveBooklet = useCallback((bookletData: Omit<RecipeBooklet, 'id' | 'createdAt' | 'updatedAt'> & { id?: string }): RecipeBooklet => {
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    if (bookletData.id) {
      const existing = booklets.find(b => b.id === bookletData.id);
      const updated: RecipeBooklet = { ...bookletData, id: bookletData.id, createdAt: existing?.createdAt || now, updatedAt: now };
      setBooklets(prev => prev.map(b => (b.id === updated.id ? updated : b)));
      return updated;
    }
    const saved: RecipeBooklet = { ...bookletData, id: `booklet-${Date.now()}`, createdAt: now, updatedAt: now };
    setBooklets(prev => [saved, ...prev]);
    return saved;
  }, [booklets]);

  const deleteBooklet = useCallback((id: string) => setBooklets(prev => prev.filter(b => b.id !== id)), []);

  const addRecipesToBooklet = useCallback((bookletId: string, recipeIdsToAdd: string[]) => {
    setBooklets(prev => prev.map(b => b.id !== bookletId ? b : {
      ...b, recipeIds: Array.from(new Set([...b.recipeIds, ...recipeIdsToAdd])), updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
    }));
  }, []);

  const [filters, setFilters] = useState<RecipeFilters>({
    search: '', dietType: 'Tümü', mealType: 'Tümü', practicality: 'Tümü',
    transportability: 'Tümü', category: 'Tümü', season: 'Tümü', status: 'Tümü'
  });

  const [isWizardOpen, setIsWizardOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingRecipe, setEditingRecipe] = useState<Recipe | null>(null);
  const [selectedRecipeForDetail, setSelectedRecipeForDetail] = useState<Recipe | null>(null);

  const openWizard = useCallback((recipeToEdit?: Recipe) => { setEditingRecipe(recipeToEdit || null); setIsWizardOpen(true); }, []);
  const closeWizard = useCallback(() => { setIsWizardOpen(false); setEditingRecipe(null); }, []);
  const openDetail = useCallback((recipe: Recipe) => setSelectedRecipeForDetail(recipe), []);
  const closeDetail = useCallback(() => setSelectedRecipeForDetail(null), []);

  const calculateIngredientNutrition = useCallback((foodOrId: FoodItem | string, amount: number, unit: string) => turKompMockService.calculateIngredient(foodOrId, amount, unit), []);
  const calculateRecipeTotals = useCallback((ingredients: RecipeIngredient[]) => turKompMockService.calculateRecipeTotals(ingredients), []);
  const recalculateRecipe = useCallback((recipe: Recipe): Recipe => turKompMockService.recalculateRecipe(recipe), []);

  const saveRecipe = useCallback((recipe: Recipe) => {
    const deterministicallyCalculatedRecipe = turKompMockService.recalculateRecipe(recipe);
    setRecipes(prev => prev.some(r => r.id === recipe.id) ? prev.map(r => (r.id === recipe.id ? deterministicallyCalculatedRecipe : r)) : [deterministicallyCalculatedRecipe, ...prev]);
    setSelectedRecipeForDetail(deterministicallyCalculatedRecipe);
  }, []);

  const deleteRecipe = useCallback((id: string) => {
    setRecipes(prev => prev.filter(r => r.id !== id));
    setSelectedRecipeForDetail(prev => (prev?.id === id ? null : prev));
  }, []);

  const addCustomFood = useCallback((food: FoodItem) => {
    const formatted = turKompMockService.addFood(food);
    setFoods(prev => [formatted, ...prev.filter(f => f.id !== formatted.id)]);
  }, []);

  const filteredRecipes = useMemo(() => {
    const q = filters.search.toLowerCase().trim();
    const filterDietLower = filters.dietType.toLowerCase();
    const filterMealLower = filters.mealType.toLowerCase();

    return recipes.filter(r => {
      if (q) {
        const match = r.dietaryFlags?.allText ? r.dietaryFlags.allText.includes(q) : r.name.toLowerCase().includes(q);
        if (!match) return false;
      }
      if (filters.dietType !== 'Tümü' && !(r.dietTypes?.some(dt => dt.toLowerCase() === filterDietLower) || r.dietType === filters.dietType)) return false;
      if (filters.mealType !== 'Tümü' && !(r.mealTypes?.some(mt => mt.toLowerCase() === filterMealLower) || r.mealType === filters.mealType)) return false;
      if (filters.practicality !== 'Tümü' && r.practicality !== filters.practicality) return false;
      if (filters.transportability !== 'Tümü' && r.transportability !== filters.transportability) return false;
      if (filters.category !== 'Tümü' && r.category !== filters.category) return false;
      if (filters.status === 'Aktif' && r.isDraft) return false;
      if (filters.status === 'Taslak' && !r.isDraft) return false;
      return true;
    });
  }, [recipes, filters]);

  const turKompStats = turKompMockService.getStats();

  const stats = useMemo(() => ({
    totalRecipes: recipes.length, activeRecipes: recipes.filter(r => !r.isDraft).length,
    draftRecipes: recipes.filter(r => r.isDraft).length, totalFoods: foods.length,
    turKompCount: turKompStats.turKompCount, usdaCount: turKompStats.usdaCount
  }), [recipes, foods.length, turKompStats.turKompCount, turKompStats.usdaCount]);

  const contextValue = useMemo(() => ({
    activeModule, setActiveModule, recipes, recipeMap, filters, setFilters, filteredRecipes, saveRecipe, deleteRecipe,
    booklets, saveBooklet, deleteBooklet, addRecipesToBooklet, isWizardOpen, editingRecipe, openWizard, closeWizard,
    selectedRecipeForDetail, openDetail, closeDetail, foods, addCustomFood, turKompService: turKompMockService,
    turKompStats, calculateIngredientNutrition, calculateRecipeTotals, recalculateRecipe, clients,
    activePlannerClientId, setActivePlannerClientId, stats
  }), [
    activeModule, recipes, recipeMap, filters, filteredRecipes, saveRecipe, deleteRecipe,
    booklets, saveBooklet, deleteBooklet, addRecipesToBooklet, isWizardOpen, editingRecipe, openWizard, closeWizard,
    selectedRecipeForDetail, openDetail, closeDetail, foods, addCustomFood, turKompStats,
    calculateIngredientNutrition, calculateRecipeTotals, recalculateRecipe, clients, activePlannerClientId, stats
  ]);

  return <NutritionContext.Provider value={contextValue}>{children}</NutritionContext.Provider>;
};

export const useNutrition = () => {
  const context = useContext(NutritionContext);
  if (!context) throw new Error('useNutrition must be used within a NutritionProvider');
  return context;
};

  id, name, category, source, foodCode, latinName, notes, defaultUnit, unitWeightGrams, [calories, protein, carbs, fat, fiber], exchangeContributionPer100g
]) => ({

  name,

  latinName,
  notes,
  defaultUnit,

  exchangeContributionPer100g
}));

// [27/41] DOSYA: src/data/turkishRecipes.ts (77 satır)

import { Recipe, RecipeIngredient, RecipeDietaryFlags } from '../types/nutrition';
import { parseAmountAndUnit } from '../services/recipePersonalizationEngine';
import { TURKISH_BREAKFAST_RECIPES } from './turkishBreakfastRecipes';
import { TURKISH_SNACK_RECIPES } from './turkishSnackRecipes';
import { TURKISH_DINNER_RECIPES } from './turkishDinnerRecipes';

export { TURKISH_BREAKFAST_RECIPES, TURKISH_SNACK_RECIPES, TURKISH_DINNER_RECIPES };

const GLUTEN_TERMS = ['buğday', 'arpa', 'çavdar', 'durum', 'kuskus', 'bulgur', 'irmik', 'makarna', 'un', 'şehriye', 'bisküvi', 'ekmek', 'yufka', 'galeta', 'simit', 'grissini', 'bazlama', 'lavaş'];
const DAIRY_TERMS = ['süt', 'yoğurt', 'peynir', 'tereyağı', 'krema', 'kaymak', 'lor', 'kaşar', 'kefir', 'labne', 'hellim', 'tulum', 'cacık', 'ayran'];
const HIGH_CARB_TERMS = ['şeker', 'bal', 'pekmez', 'pirinç', 'makarna', 'patates', 'muz', 'ekmek', 'un', 'bulgur', 'mısır', 'kinoa', 'simit', 'nohut', 'fasulye', 'mercimek'];
const ELIM_TRIGGERS = ['buğday', 'arpa', 'çavdar', 'un', 'süt', 'peynir', 'yoğurt', 'tereyağı', 'krema', 'yumurta', 'soya', 'patlıcan', 'yer fıstığı', 'mısır', 'işlenmiş'];
const HIGH_FODMAP = ['soğan', 'sarımsak', 'pırasa', 'karnabahar', 'mantar', 'kuru fasulye', 'nohut', 'mercimek', 'bakla', 'elma', 'armut', 'karpuz', 'bal'];
const HIGH_HISTAMINE = ['eski kaşar', 'parmesan', 'rokfor', 'tulum', 'konserve', 'ton balığı', 'salamura', 'turşu', 'sirke', 'ıspanak', 'domates', 'avokado', 'patlıcan', 'şarküteri', 'sosis', 'sucuk', 'salam'];
const ANTI_FERTILITY = ['margarin', 'rafine şeker', 'işlenmiş', 'sosis', 'salam', 'kızartma'];
const MEAT_TERMS = ['et', 'dana', 'kuzu', 'tavuk', 'balık', 'köfte', 'hindi', 'kıyma', 'somon', 'levrek', 'çipura', 'ton balığı', 'karides', 'sakatat', 'ciğer', 'bonfile', 'antrikot', 'sucuk'];

export function computeRecipeDietaryFlags(recipe: Recipe): RecipeDietaryFlags {
  const rName = (recipe.name || '').toLowerCase();
  const ingList = recipe.ingredients?.length ? recipe.ingredients.map(i => i.foodName.toLowerCase()) : (recipe.detailedIngredients || []).map(di => di.name.toLowerCase());
  const allText = `${rName} ${(recipe.category || '').toLowerCase()} ${(recipe.family || '').toLowerCase()} ${(recipe.description || '').toLowerCase()} ${(recipe.kitchenNotes || '').toLowerCase()} ${(recipe.clinicalNote || '').toLowerCase()} ${ingList.join(' ')} ${(recipe.tags || []).join(' ').toLowerCase()}`;

  return {
    hasGluten: ingList.some(ing => GLUTEN_TERMS.some(t => ing.includes(t) && !ing.includes('glutensiz'))),
    hasDairy: ingList.some(ing => DAIRY_TERMS.some(t => ing.includes(t) && !/laktozsuz|bitkisel|badem|hindistan cevizi|soya sütü/.test(ing))),
    hasHighCarb: ingList.some(ing => HIGH_CARB_TERMS.some(t => ing.includes(t) && !ing.includes('unsuz') && !ing.includes('şekersiz'))),
    hasElimTrigger: ingList.some(ing => ELIM_TRIGGERS.some(t => ing.includes(t) && !ing.includes('glutensiz') && !ing.includes('laktozsuz'))),
    hasHighFodmap: ingList.some(ing => HIGH_FODMAP.some(t => ing.includes(t))),
    hasHighHistamine: ingList.some(ing => HIGH_HISTAMINE.some(t => ing.includes(t))),
    hasAntiFertility: ingList.some(ing => ANTI_FERTILITY.some(t => ing.includes(t))),
    hasMeat: ingList.some(ing => MEAT_TERMS.some(t => ing.includes(t))) || MEAT_TERMS.some(t => rName.includes(t) && !rName.includes('etsiz')),
    allText
  };
}

export function getUnifiedRecipeIngredients(recipe: Recipe): RecipeIngredient[] {
  if (recipe.ingredients?.length) return recipe.ingredients;
  if (recipe.detailedIngredients?.length) {
    return recipe.detailedIngredients.map((di, idx) => {
      const parsed = parseAmountAndUnit(di.amountDisplay);
      return {
        id: `${recipe.id}_ing_${idx + 1}`, foodId: di.foodId || `food_custom_${idx + 1}`,
        foodName: di.name, amount: parsed.amount, unit: parsed.unit, amountDisplay: di.amountDisplay,
        calculatedCalories: 0, calculatedProtein: 0, calculatedCarbs: 0, calculatedFat: 0, calculatedFiber: 0
      };
    });
  }
  return [];
}

export function hydrateRecipeIngredients(recipe: Recipe): Recipe {
  let hydrated = recipe;
  if (!recipe.ingredients?.length && recipe.detailedIngredients?.length) {
    const count = recipe.detailedIngredients.length;
    const { calories = 250, protein = 12, carbs = 25, fat = 10, fiber = 3 } = recipe.totalNutrition || {};
    hydrated = {
      ...recipe,
      ingredients: recipe.detailedIngredients.map((di, idx) => {
        const parsed = parseAmountAndUnit(di.amountDisplay);
        return {
          id: `${recipe.id}_ing_${idx + 1}`, foodId: di.foodId || `food_custom_${idx + 1}`,
          foodName: di.name, amount: parsed.amount, unit: parsed.unit, amountDisplay: di.amountDisplay,
          calculatedCalories: Math.round(calories / count), calculatedProtein: Number((protein / count).toFixed(1)),
          calculatedCarbs: Number((carbs / count).toFixed(1)), calculatedFat: Number((fat / count).toFixed(1)),
          calculatedFiber: Number((fiber / count).toFixed(1))
        };
      })
    };
  }
  return { ...hydrated, dietaryFlags: computeRecipeDietaryFlags(hydrated) };
}

export const TURKISH_RECIPES: Recipe[] = [
  ...TURKISH_BREAKFAST_RECIPES,
  ...TURKISH_SNACK_RECIPES,
  ...TURKISH_DINNER_RECIPES
].map(hydrateRecipeIngredients);

// [31/41] DOSYA: src/services/html2pdfShim.ts (11 satır)

const html2pdfShim = () => {
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && (window as any).html2pdf) return (window as any).html2pdf();
  return {
    set: () => ({
      from: (_element: HTMLElement) => ({
        save: async () => { if (typeof window !== 'undefined') window.print(); }
      })
    })
  };
};
export default html2pdfShim;

// [32/41] DOSYA: src/services/menuPlanningEngine.ts (729 satır)

import { Recipe, RecipeExchanges, RecipeTotalNutrition, DietType } from '../types/nutrition';
import { computeRecipeDietaryFlags, getUnifiedRecipeIngredients } from '../data/turkishRecipes';
import {
  BMRFormula, CalorieArchetypeLevel, ArchetypePreset, PlannerClientData, MealPoolOption,
  MealRole, SelectedMealConfig, DayOfWeek, DayScheduleConfig, WeeklyMenuPlan,
  PlannedDayMenu, PlannedMealItem, RecipeVarietyStrategy, MealAlternativeOption,
  DailyMacroTargetGrams, MacroRatioConfig
} from '../types/menuPlanner';
import { personalizeRecipeForMeal, detectIngredientCategory } from './recipePersonalizationEngine';
import { calculateTargetMacroGrams, DEFAULT_MACRO_RATIOS, validateRecipeIntegrity, evaluateAlternativeCategory, checkMacroCompliance } from '../utils/nutritionCalculator';

export const DAYS_OF_WEEK: DayOfWeek[] = ['Pazartesi', 'Salı', 'Çarşamba', 'Perşembe', 'Cuma', 'Cumartesi', 'Pazar'];
export const CANONICAL_MEAL_ORDER: MealPoolOption[] = ['Sabah', 'Kahvaltı', 'Kuşluk', 'Öğle', 'İkindi', 'Akşam', 'Gece'];

export function sortMealsByCanonicalOrder<T extends { name?: MealPoolOption; mealName?: MealPoolOption }>(meals: T[]): T[] {
  return [...meals].sort((a, b) => {
    const idxA = CANONICAL_MEAL_ORDER.indexOf((a.name || a.mealName || 'Sabah') as MealPoolOption);
    const idxB = CANONICAL_MEAL_ORDER.indexOf((b.name || b.mealName || 'Sabah') as MealPoolOption);
    return (idxA !== -1 ? idxA : 99) - (idxB !== -1 ? idxB : 99);
  });
}

export function autoDistributeMealPercentages(meals: SelectedMealConfig[]): SelectedMealConfig[] {
  if (meals.length === 0) return [];
  if (meals.length === 1) return [{ ...meals[0], percentage: 100 }];

  const mainCount = meals.filter(m => m.role === 'Ana Öğün').length;
  const snackCount = meals.filter(m => m.role === 'Ara Öğün').length;
  const allocated: Record<string, number> = {};

  if (mainCount === 3 && snackCount === 2) {
    meals.forEach(m => {
      allocated[m.name] = m.name === 'Öğle' ? 30 : m.name === 'Kahvaltı' || m.name === 'Akşam' ? 25 : m.role === 'Ana Öğün' ? 25 : 10;
    });
  } else if (mainCount === 3 && snackCount === 1) {
    meals.forEach(m => {
      allocated[m.name] = m.name === 'Kahvaltı' ? 30 : m.name === 'Öğle' ? 35 : m.name === 'Akşam' ? 25 : m.role === 'Ara Öğün' ? 10 : 25;
    });
  } else if (mainCount === 3 && snackCount === 0) {
    meals.forEach(m => { allocated[m.name] = m.name === 'Kahvaltı' || m.name === 'Öğle' ? 35 : m.name === 'Akşam' ? 30 : 33; });
  } else if (mainCount === 2 && snackCount === 1) {
    meals.forEach(m => { allocated[m.name] = m.role === 'Ana Öğün' ? 40 : 20; });
  } else if (mainCount === 2 && snackCount === 0) {
    meals.forEach(m => { allocated[m.name] = 50; });
  } else if (mainCount === 2 && snackCount === 2) {
    meals.forEach(m => { allocated[m.name] = m.role === 'Ana Öğün' ? 35 : 15; });
  } else {
    const weights = meals.map(m => (m.role === 'Ana Öğün' ? 3.0 : 1.0));
    const totalW = weights.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    const pcts = meals.map((_, idx) => Math.max(5, Math.round(((weights[idx] / totalW) * 100) / 5) * 5));
    const diff = 100 - pcts.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    if (diff !== 0) {
      let maxIdx = 0;
      for (let i = 1; i < pcts.length; i++) if (pcts[i] > pcts[maxIdx]) maxIdx = i;
      pcts[maxIdx] += diff;
    }
    meals.forEach((m, idx) => { allocated[m.name] = pcts[idx]; });
  }

  const currentTotal = Object.values(allocated).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
  if (currentTotal !== 100 && meals.length > 0) {
    const firstMain = meals.find(m => m.role === 'Ana Öğün') || meals[0];
    allocated[firstMain.name] = (allocated[firstMain.name] || 0) + (100 - currentTotal);
  }
  return meals.map(m => ({ ...m, percentage: allocated[m.name] !== undefined ? allocated[m.name] : Math.round(100 / meals.length) }));
}

export const MEAL_POOL_OPTIONS: { name: MealPoolOption; defaultRole: MealRole; defaultPercentage: number }[] = [
  { name: 'Sabah', defaultRole: 'Ara Öğün', defaultPercentage: 10 },
  { name: 'Kahvaltı', defaultRole: 'Ana Öğün', defaultPercentage: 25 },
  { name: 'Kuşluk', defaultRole: 'Ara Öğün', defaultPercentage: 10 },
  { name: 'Öğle', defaultRole: 'Ana Öğün', defaultPercentage: 30 },
  { name: 'İkindi', defaultRole: 'Ara Öğün', defaultPercentage: 10 },
  { name: 'Akşam', defaultRole: 'Ana Öğün', defaultPercentage: 25 },
  { name: 'Gece', defaultRole: 'Ara Öğün', defaultPercentage: 10 }
];

const dist = (percentage: number, protein: number, carb: number, fat: number, vegetable: number) => ({
  percentage, exchanges: { protein, carb, fat, vegetable }
});

export function calculateBMR(gender: 'Kadın' | 'Erkek', weightKg: number, heightCm: number, age: number, formula: BMRFormula = 'mifflin_st_jeor'): number {
  if (formula === 'mifflin_st_jeor') {
    const base = 10 * weightKg + 6.25 * heightCm - 5 * age;
    return Math.round(gender === 'Erkek' ? base + 5 : base - 161);
  }
  return gender === 'Erkek'
    ? Math.round(88.362 + 13.397 * weightKg + 4.799 * heightCm - 5.677 * age)
    : Math.round(447.593 + 9.247 * weightKg + 3.098 * heightCm - 4.330 * age);
}

export const PAL_FACTORS: Record<string, { factor: number; label: string; desc: string }> = {
  'Sedanter (Masa Başı, Sıfır Egzersiz)': { factor: 1.0, label: 'Sedanter', desc: 'Masa başı iş, minimum hareket (*1.0)' },
  'Sedanter (Masa Başı)': { factor: 1.0, label: 'Sedanter', desc: 'Masa başı iş, minimum hareket (*1.0)' },
  'Hafif Aktif (1-2 Gün Spor)': { factor: 1.1, label: 'Hafif Aktif', desc: 'Haftada 1-2 gün yürüyüş / egzersiz (*1.1)' },
  'Orta Derece Aktif (3-5 Gün Spor)': { factor: 1.2, label: 'Orta Aktif', desc: 'Haftada 3-5 gün ortalama egzersiz (*1.2)' },
  'Orta Düzey (3-4 Gün Spor)': { factor: 1.2, label: 'Orta Aktif', desc: 'Haftada 3-4 gün ortalama egzersiz (*1.2)' },
  'Yüksek Aktivite (5+ Gün)': { factor: 1.4, label: 'Çok Aktif', desc: 'Haftada 5+ gün yoğun spor (*1.4)' },
  'Çok Aktif (6-7 Gün Ağır Spor)': { factor: 1.4, label: 'Çok Aktif', desc: 'Haftada 6-7 gün ağır antrenman (*1.4)' }
};

export function getPALFactor(activityLevel: string): number {
  if (!activityLevel) return 1.1;
  if (PAL_FACTORS[activityLevel]) return PAL_FACTORS[activityLevel].factor;
  const lower = activityLevel.toLowerCase();
  if (lower.includes('sedanter') || lower.includes('masa')) return 1.0;
  if (lower.includes('hafif')) return 1.1;
  if (lower.includes('orta')) return 1.2;
  if (lower.includes('yüksek') || lower.includes('çok')) return 1.4;
  return 1.1;
}

export function calculateTargetEnergyFromPAL(bmr: number, activityLevel: string, weightKg?: number, targetWeightKg?: number) {
  const pal = getPALFactor(activityLevel);
  const tdee = Math.round(bmr * pal);
  let deficitOrSurplus = -350;
  if (targetWeightKg && weightKg && weightKg > 0) {
    const diff = targetWeightKg - weightKg;
    deficitOrSurplus = diff < -5 ? -500 : diff < -0.5 ? -350 : diff > 5 ? 500 : diff > 0.5 ? 350 : 0;
  }
  const minSafetyFloor = Math.max(1000, Math.min(bmr, 1200));
  return { tdee, pal, targetEnergy: Math.round(Math.max(minSafetyFloor, tdee + deficitOrSurplus)), deficitOrSurplus };
}

export interface BMIAnalysis {
  bmi: number; category: string; categoryColor: string; badgeBg: string;
  idealWeightMin: number; idealWeightMax: number; targetBmi?: number; targetBmiCategory?: string; weightDifference?: number;
}

export function calculateBMI(weightKg: number, heightCm: number, targetWeightKg?: number): BMIAnalysis {
  const safeHeight = heightCm > 0 ? heightCm : 165;
  const safeWeight = weightKg > 0 ? weightKg : 65;
  const heightM = safeHeight / 100;
  const bmi = Number((safeWeight / (heightM * heightM)).toFixed(1));

  let category = 'Normal / İdeal Kilolu', categoryColor = 'text-emerald-400', badgeBg = 'bg-emerald-950/80 text-emerald-200 border-emerald-700';
  if (bmi < 18.5) { category = 'Zayıf (Düşük Kilolu)'; categoryColor = 'text-sky-400'; badgeBg = 'bg-sky-950/80 text-sky-200 border-sky-700'; }
  else if (bmi >= 25 && bmi < 30) { category = 'Fazla Kilolu (Hafif Şişman)'; categoryColor = 'text-amber-400'; badgeBg = 'bg-amber-950/80 text-amber-200 border-amber-700'; }
  else if (bmi >= 30 && bmi < 35) { category = '1. Derece Obezite'; categoryColor = 'text-orange-400'; badgeBg = 'bg-orange-950/80 text-orange-200 border-orange-700'; }
  else if (bmi >= 35 && bmi < 40) { category = '2. Derece Obezite'; categoryColor = 'text-rose-400'; badgeBg = 'bg-rose-950/80 text-rose-200 border-rose-700'; }
  else if (bmi >= 40) { category = '3. Derece Morbid Obezite'; categoryColor = 'text-red-400'; badgeBg = 'bg-red-950/80 text-red-200 border-red-700'; }

  const idealWeightMin = Number((18.5 * heightM * heightM).toFixed(1));
  const idealWeightMax = Number((24.9 * heightM * heightM).toFixed(1));
  let targetBmi: number | undefined, targetBmiCategory: string | undefined, weightDifference: number | undefined;

  if (targetWeightKg && targetWeightKg > 0) {
    targetBmi = Number((targetWeightKg / (heightM * heightM)).toFixed(1));
    weightDifference = Number((targetWeightKg - safeWeight).toFixed(1));
    targetBmiCategory = targetBmi < 18.5 ? 'Zayıf' : targetBmi < 25 ? 'Normal / İdeal' : targetBmi < 30 ? 'Fazla Kilolu' : 'Obezite';
  }
  return { bmi, category, categoryColor, badgeBg, idealWeightMin, idealWeightMax, targetBmi, targetBmiCategory, weightDifference };
}

export function determineDefaultArchetype(targetEnergy: number): { archetype: CalorieArchetypeLevel; isBelowMinimumSafety: boolean } {
  const levels: CalorieArchetypeLevel[] = ['XS1000', 'S1200', 'M1400', 'L1600', 'XL1800'];
  let closest = levels[0], minDiff = Infinity;
  for (const lvl of levels) {
    const diff = Math.abs(targetEnergy - ARCHETYPE_PRESETS[lvl].targetCalories);
    if (diff < minDiff) { minDiff = diff; closest = lvl; }
  }
  return { archetype: closest, isBelowMinimumSafety: targetEnergy < 1000 };
}

export function isRecipeCompatibleWithDiet(recipe: Recipe, dietCriteria: DietType | DietType[], excludedAllergies: string[] = []): boolean {
  const criteriaList = Array.isArray(dietCriteria) ? dietCriteria : [dietCriteria];
  const rDietTypes = recipe.dietTypes?.length ? recipe.dietTypes : [recipe.dietType || 'STD'];
  const rTags = (recipe.tags || []).map(t => t.toLowerCase());
  const rCategory = (recipe.category || '').toLowerCase();
  const flags = recipe.dietaryFlags || computeRecipeDietaryFlags(recipe);

  for (const criterion of criteriaList) {
    if (!criterion || criterion === 'STD' || criterion === 'Standart (STD)') continue;
    if (criterion === 'GF' || criterion === 'Glutensiz (GF)') {
      if (!(rDietTypes.some(d => ['GF', 'Glutensiz (GF)', 'GF + LF', 'GF+LF'].includes(d)) || rTags.includes('glutensiz') || rTags.includes('gf')) && flags.hasGluten) return false;
    } else if (criterion === 'LF' || criterion === 'Laktozsuz (LF)') {
      if (!(rDietTypes.some(d => ['LF', 'Laktozsuz (LF)', 'GF + LF', 'GF+LF'].includes(d)) || rTags.includes('laktozsuz') || rTags.includes('lf')) && flags.hasDairy) return false;
    } else if (criterion === 'GF + LF' || criterion === 'GF+LF') {
      const isGF = rDietTypes.some(d => ['GF', 'Glutensiz (GF)', 'GF + LF', 'GF+LF'].includes(d)) || rTags.includes('glutensiz');
      const isLF = rDietTypes.some(d => ['LF', 'Laktozsuz (LF)', 'GF + LF', 'GF+LF'].includes(d)) || rTags.includes('laktozsuz');
      if ((!isGF || !isLF) && (flags.hasGluten || flags.hasDairy)) return false;
    } else if (criterion === 'Ketojenik') {
      const isKeto = rDietTypes.includes('Ketojenik') || rDietTypes.includes('Keto' as any) || rCategory.includes('keto') || rTags.includes('keto') || rTags.includes('ketojenik');
      if (!isKeto && ((recipe.totalNutrition?.carbs || 0) > 20 || flags.hasHighCarb)) return false;
    } else if (criterion === 'Eliminasyon (4\'lü)' || criterion === 'Eliminasyon') {
      if (!(rDietTypes.some(d => ['Eliminasyon (4\'lü)', 'Eliminasyon'].includes(d)) || rTags.includes('eliminasyon')) && flags.hasElimTrigger) return false;
    } else if (criterion === 'Düşük FODMAP' || criterion === 'FODMAP') {
      if (!(rDietTypes.some(d => ['Düşük FODMAP', 'FODMAP', 'Low FODMAP'].includes(d)) || rTags.includes('fodmap') || rTags.includes('düşük fodmap')) && flags.hasHighFodmap) return false;
    } else if (criterion === 'Histamin') {
      if (!(rDietTypes.includes('Histamin') || rTags.includes('histamin') || rTags.includes('düşük histamin')) && flags.hasHighHistamine) return false;
    } else if (criterion === 'Fertilite') {
      if (flags.hasAntiFertility) return false;
    } else if (criterion === 'Vejetaryen') {
      if (!(rDietTypes.includes('Vejetaryen') || rTags.includes('vejetaryen')) && flags.hasMeat) return false;
    }
  }

  for (const allergy of excludedAllergies) {
    const clean = allergy.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!clean) continue;
    if (clean === 'gluten' && flags.hasGluten) return false;
    if ((clean === 'laktoz' || clean === 'süt') && flags.hasDairy) return false;
    if (clean === 'yumurta' && /yumurta|omlet|menemen|çılbır|frittata/.test(flags.allText)) return false;
    if ((clean === 'balık' || clean === 'deniz ürünleri' || clean === 'kabuklu deniz ürünleri') && /balık|somon|levrek|çipura|ton balığı|karides|kalamar|hamsi|sardalya|ahtapot/.test(flags.allText)) return false;
    if ((clean === 'fıstık' || clean === 'yer fıstığı') && /fıstık/.test(flags.allText)) return false;
    if ((clean === 'fındık / ceviz' || clean === 'kuruyemiş' || clean === 'fındık' || clean === 'ceviz') && /fındık|ceviz|badem|kaju|antep fıstığı/.test(flags.allText)) return false;
    if (clean === 'soya' && /soya|tofu|edamame/.test(flags.allText)) return false;
    if (clean === 'susam' && /susam|tahin|simit/.test(flags.allText)) return false;
    if (flags.allText.includes(clean)) return false;
  }
  return true;
}

export function isRecipeMealTypeMatch(recipe: Recipe, mealName: MealPoolOption): boolean {
  const rMeal = recipe.mealType, rMeals = recipe.mealTypes || [rMeal], rCat = recipe.category || '';
  if (mealName === 'Sabah' || mealName === 'Kahvaltı') return rMeals.includes('Kahvaltı') || rCat.includes('Kahvalt') || rMeal === 'Kahvaltı';
  if (mealName === 'Kuşluk' || mealName === 'İkindi' || mealName === 'Gece') return rMeals.includes('Ara Öğün') || rCat.includes('Ara Öğün') || rCat.includes('Tatlı') || rCat.includes('İçecek') || rMeal === 'Ara Öğün';
  if (mealName === 'Öğle' || mealName === 'Akşam') return rMeals.includes('Öğle') || rMeals.includes('Akşam') || rCat.includes('Ana Yemek') || rCat.includes('Çorba') || rCat.includes('Salata') || rMeal === 'Öğle' || rMeal === 'Akşam';
  return true;
}

export interface AdaptedRecipeResult {
  adaptedRecipe: Recipe | null; calculatedNutrition: RecipeTotalNutrition; calculatedExchanges: RecipeExchanges; portionMultiplier: number;
}

export function adaptRecipeToArchetypeAndMeal(
  recipe: Recipe | null,
  archetypeLevel: CalorieArchetypeLevel,
  mealTargetCalories: number,
  _mealTargetExchanges?: { protein: number; carb: number; fat: number; vegetable?: number; fruit?: number; dairy?: number },
  mealTargetMacros?: { calories: number; proteinG: number; carbG: number; fatG: number }
): AdaptedRecipeResult {
  if (!recipe) {
    return {
      adaptedRecipe: null,
      calculatedNutrition: { calories: 0, protein: 0, carbs: 0, fat: 0, fiber: 0 },
      calculatedExchanges: { protein: 0, carb: 0, fat: 0, vegetable: 0, fruit: 0, dairy: 0 },
      portionMultiplier: 1
    };
  }

  const explicit = recipe.archetypeAdaptations?.[archetypeLevel];
  const baseCalories = recipe.totalNutrition?.calories || 300;
  const baseProtein = recipe.totalNutrition?.protein || 15;
  const baseCarbs = recipe.totalNutrition?.carbs || 30;
  const baseFat = recipe.totalNutrition?.fat || 10;

  let overallMultiplier = 1;
  if (explicit && explicit.portionMultiplier > 0) overallMultiplier = explicit.portionMultiplier;
  else if (baseCalories > 0 && mealTargetCalories > 0) overallMultiplier = Math.max(0.5, Math.min(2.5, Number((mealTargetCalories / baseCalories).toFixed(2))));

  let pMult = overallMultiplier, cMult = overallMultiplier, fMult = overallMultiplier;
  if (mealTargetMacros && baseCalories > 0) {
    if (baseProtein > 0 && mealTargetMacros.proteinG > 0) pMult = Math.max(0.55, Math.min(2.2, Number(((mealTargetMacros.proteinG / baseProtein) * 0.75 + overallMultiplier * 0.25).toFixed(2))));
    if (baseCarbs > 0 && mealTargetMacros.carbG > 0) cMult = Math.max(0.55, Math.min(2.2, Number(((mealTargetMacros.carbG / baseCarbs) * 0.75 + overallMultiplier * 0.25).toFixed(2))));
    if (baseFat > 0 && mealTargetMacros.fatG > 0) fMult = Math.max(0.5, Math.min(2.0, Number(((mealTargetMacros.fatG / baseFat) * 0.75 + overallMultiplier * 0.25).toFixed(2))));
  }

  const baseIngredients = recipe.ingredients?.length ? recipe.ingredients : getUnifiedRecipeIngredients(recipe);
  const adaptedIngredients = baseIngredients.map(ing => {
    const category = detectIngredientCategory(ing.foodName || '');
    const ingMultiplier = category === 'protein' ? pMult : category === 'carb' ? cMult : category === 'fat' ? fMult : overallMultiplier;
    return {
      ...ing,
      amount: Number((ing.amount * ingMultiplier).toFixed(1)),
      calculatedCalories: Math.round((ing.calculatedCalories || 0) * ingMultiplier),
      calculatedProtein: Number(((ing.calculatedProtein || 0) * ingMultiplier).toFixed(1)),
      calculatedCarbs: Number(((ing.calculatedCarbs || 0) * ingMultiplier).toFixed(1)),
      calculatedFat: Number(((ing.calculatedFat || 0) * ingMultiplier).toFixed(1)),
      calculatedFiber: Number(((ing.calculatedFiber || 0) * ingMultiplier).toFixed(1))
    };
  });

  let sumCal = 0, sumP = 0, sumC = 0, sumF = 0, sumFib = 0;
  if (adaptedIngredients.length > 0) {
    for (const ing of adaptedIngredients) {
      sumCal += ing.calculatedCalories || 0; sumP += ing.calculatedProtein || 0;
      sumC += ing.calculatedCarbs || 0; sumF += ing.calculatedFat || 0; sumFib += ing.calculatedFiber || 0;
    }
  } else {
    sumCal = Math.round(baseCalories * overallMultiplier); sumP = Number((baseProtein * pMult).toFixed(1));
    sumC = Number((baseCarbs * cMult).toFixed(1)); sumF = Number((baseFat * fMult).toFixed(1));
    sumFib = Number(((recipe.totalNutrition?.fiber || 3) * overallMultiplier).toFixed(1));
  }

  if (mealTargetCalories > 0 && Math.abs(sumCal - mealTargetCalories) > mealTargetCalories * 0.08) {
    const energyNormFactor = mealTargetCalories / Math.max(50, sumCal);
    sumCal = Math.round(sumCal * energyNormFactor); sumP = Number((sumP * energyNormFactor).toFixed(1));
    sumC = Number((sumC * energyNormFactor).toFixed(1)); sumF = Number((sumF * energyNormFactor).toFixed(1));
  }

  const calculatedNutrition: RecipeTotalNutrition = {
    calories: Math.round(sumCal), protein: Number(sumP.toFixed(1)), carbs: Number(sumC.toFixed(1)), fat: Number(sumF.toFixed(1)), fiber: Number(sumFib.toFixed(1))
  };
  const calculatedExchanges: RecipeExchanges = explicit?.adaptedExchanges || {
    protein: Number(((recipe.exchanges?.protein || 2) * pMult).toFixed(1)),
    carb: Number(((recipe.exchanges?.carb || 2) * cMult).toFixed(1)),
    fat: Number(((recipe.exchanges?.fat || 1) * fMult).toFixed(1)),
    vegetable: Number(((recipe.exchanges?.vegetable || 1) * overallMultiplier).toFixed(1)),
    fruit: Number(((recipe.exchanges?.fruit || 0) * overallMultiplier).toFixed(1)),
    dairy: Number(((recipe.exchanges?.dairy || 0) * overallMultiplier).toFixed(1))
  };
  const portionDesc = overallMultiplier === 1 ? (recipe.portionDescription || '1 Porsiyon') : `${overallMultiplier.toFixed(2).replace(/\.00$/, '')} Porsiyon (${archetypeLevel} Arketip Uyarlamalı)`;

  return {
    adaptedRecipe: { ...recipe, portionDescription: portionDesc, totalNutrition: calculatedNutrition, exchanges: calculatedExchanges, ingredients: adaptedIngredients },
    calculatedNutrition, calculatedExchanges, portionMultiplier: overallMultiplier
  };
}

export function generateMealAlternatives(
  primaryRecipe: Recipe | null,
  mealName: MealPoolOption,
  mealTargetCalories: number,
  mealTargetMacros: { calories: number; proteinG: number; carbG: number; fatG: number }
): MealAlternativeOption[] {
  const primaryName = primaryRecipe?.name || 'Ana Menü';
  const primaryCal = primaryRecipe?.totalNutrition?.calories || mealTargetCalories;
  const primaryP = primaryRecipe?.totalNutrition?.protein || mealTargetMacros.proteinG;
  const primaryC = primaryRecipe?.totalNutrition?.carbs || mealTargetMacros.carbG;
  const primaryF = primaryRecipe?.totalNutrition?.fat || mealTargetMacros.fatG;
  const baseItem = { name: primaryName, calories: primaryCal, protein: primaryP, carbs: primaryC, fat: primaryF };

  const results: MealAlternativeOption[] = [{
    id: `alt_primary_${primaryRecipe?.id || 'main'}`, name: primaryName, type: 'equivalent',
    calories: Math.round(primaryCal), protein: Number(primaryP.toFixed(1)), carbs: Number(primaryC.toFixed(1)),
    fat: Number(primaryF.toFixed(1)), fiber: Number((primaryRecipe?.totalNutrition?.fiber || 3).toFixed(1)), grams: 200, isSelected: true
  }];

  if (mealName === 'Kahvaltı' || mealName === 'Sabah') {
    const eggCount = Math.max(1, Math.round(mealTargetMacros.proteinG * 0.05));
    const breadSlices = Math.max(1, Math.round(mealTargetMacros.carbG / 20));
    const cheeseAlt = {
      name: `${eggCount} Haşlanmış Yumurta + 60g Beyaz Peynir + ${breadSlices} Dilim Ekmek`,
      calories: Math.round(mealTargetCalories * 0.98), protein: Number((mealTargetMacros.proteinG * 1.05).toFixed(1)),
      carbs: Number((mealTargetMacros.carbG * 0.95).toFixed(1)), fat: Number((mealTargetMacros.fatG * 0.92).toFixed(1))
    };
    const cat1 = evaluateAlternativeCategory(baseItem, cheeseAlt);
    results.push({ id: 'alt_bf_cheese', ...cheeseAlt, type: cat1.type, warning: cat1.warning, fiber: 4, grams: 220, isSelected: false });
  } else if (mealName === 'Öğle' || mealName === 'Akşam') {
    const meatGrams = Math.round(mealTargetMacros.proteinG * 4.2);
    const grainSpoons = Math.max(3, Math.round(mealTargetMacros.carbG / 6));
    const turkeyAlt = {
      name: `${meatGrams}g Izgara Hindi Göğsü + ${grainSpoons} yk Bulgur Pilavı + Salata`,
      calories: Math.round(mealTargetCalories * 0.99), protein: Number((mealTargetMacros.proteinG * 1.02).toFixed(1)),
      carbs: Number((mealTargetMacros.carbG * 0.98).toFixed(1)), fat: Number((mealTargetMacros.fatG * 0.9).toFixed(1))
    };
    const cat1 = evaluateAlternativeCategory(baseItem, turkeyAlt);
    results.push({ id: 'alt_main_turkey', ...turkeyAlt, type: cat1.type, warning: cat1.warning, fiber: 5, grams: 300, isSelected: false });
  } else {
    const nutAlt = {
      name: '15 Adet Çiğ Badem + 1 Porsiyon Yeşil Elma',
      calories: Math.round(mealTargetCalories), protein: Number((mealTargetMacros.proteinG * 0.9).toFixed(1)),
      carbs: Number((mealTargetMacros.carbG * 1.05).toFixed(1)), fat: Number((mealTargetMacros.fatG * 1.02).toFixed(1))
    };
    const cat1 = evaluateAlternativeCategory(baseItem, nutAlt);
    results.push({ id: 'alt_snack_nuts', ...nutAlt, type: cat1.type, warning: cat1.warning, fiber: 4, grams: 140, isSelected: false });
  }
  return results;
}

function recalculateDayTotals(dayMenu: PlannedDayMenu, targetDailyMacros: DailyMacroTargetGrams, calorieTolerancePercent = 3): PlannedDayMenu {
  let sumCal = 0, sumProt = 0, sumCarb = 0, sumFat = 0, sumFib = 0;
  let exP = 0, exC = 0, exF = 0, exV = 0, exFr = 0, exD = 0;

  for (const m of dayMenu.meals) {
    sumCal += m.calculatedNutrition?.calories || 0; sumProt += m.calculatedNutrition?.protein || 0;
    sumCarb += m.calculatedNutrition?.carbs || 0; sumFat += m.calculatedNutrition?.fat || 0; sumFib += m.calculatedNutrition?.fiber || 0;
    exP += m.calculatedExchanges?.protein || 0; exC += m.calculatedExchanges?.carb || 0; exF += m.calculatedExchanges?.fat || 0;
    exV += m.calculatedExchanges?.vegetable || 0; exFr += m.calculatedExchanges?.fruit || 0; exD += m.calculatedExchanges?.dairy || 0;
  }

  const totalEx: RecipeExchanges = {
    protein: Number(exP.toFixed(1)), carb: Number(exC.toFixed(1)), fat: Number(exF.toFixed(1)),
    vegetable: Number(exV.toFixed(1)), fruit: Number(exFr.toFixed(1)), dairy: Number(exD.toFixed(1))
  };
  const compliance = checkMacroCompliance(targetDailyMacros, { calories: sumCal, protein: sumProt, carbs: sumCarb, fat: sumFat });

  return {
    ...dayMenu,
    totalCalories: Math.round(sumCal),
    calorieDiff: Math.round(sumCal - targetDailyMacros.calories),
    totalNutrition: { calories: Math.round(sumCal), protein: Math.round(sumProt), carbs: Math.round(sumCarb), fat: Math.round(sumFat), fiber: Math.round(sumFib) },
    totalExchanges: totalEx,
    targetMacros: targetDailyMacros,
    macroDiff: {
      proteinG: Number((sumProt - targetDailyMacros.proteinG).toFixed(1)),
      carbG: Number((sumCarb - targetDailyMacros.carbG).toFixed(1)),
      fatG: Number((sumFat - targetDailyMacros.fatG).toFixed(1)),
      fiberG: Number((sumFib - targetDailyMacros.fiberG).toFixed(1))
    },
    macroCompliance: compliance,
    validationStatus: { isCalorieWithinTolerance: compliance.isEnergyCompliant, calorieTolerancePercent, messages: compliance.messages, isValid: compliance.isAllCompliant }
  };
}

export function optimizeDayMenuMacros(
  dayMenu: PlannedDayMenu,
  targetDailyMacros: DailyMacroTargetGrams,
  _allRecipes: Recipe[],
  client: PlannerClientData,
  maxPasses = 8
): PlannedDayMenu {
  let currentDay = recalculateDayTotals({ ...dayMenu, meals: [...dayMenu.meals] }, targetDailyMacros, dayMenu.validationStatus?.calorieTolerancePercent || 3);
  if (currentDay.macroCompliance?.isAllCompliant) return currentDay;

  const mainMealsIndices = currentDay.meals.map((m, idx) => ({ m, idx })).filter(item => item.m.mealRole === 'Ana Öğün').map(item => item.idx);

  for (let pass = 0; pass < maxPasses; pass++) {
    if (currentDay.macroCompliance?.isAllCompliant) break;
    const targetMealIdx = mainMealsIndices[pass % mainMealsIndices.length] ?? 0;
    const targetMeal = currentDay.meals[targetMealIdx];

    if (targetMeal?.selectedRecipe) {
      const mealTargetCal = targetMeal.targetCalories;
      const mealNormPct = targetMeal.targetPercentage || 25;
      const mealTargetM = {
        calories: mealTargetCal,
        proteinG: Number((targetDailyMacros.proteinG * (mealNormPct / 100)).toFixed(1)),
        carbG: Number((targetDailyMacros.carbG * (mealNormPct / 100)).toFixed(1)),
        fatG: Number((targetDailyMacros.fatG * (mealNormPct / 100)).toFixed(1))
      };
      const adapted = adaptRecipeToArchetypeAndMeal(targetMeal.selectedRecipe, client.selectedArchetype, mealTargetCal, undefined, mealTargetM);
      const syncedPersonalizedCopy = personalizeRecipeForMeal(
        targetMeal.selectedRecipe, adapted.calculatedExchanges, { fullName: client.fullName },
        `plan_${client.fullName}_${currentDay.day}`, targetMeal.mealName, adapted.calculatedNutrition, adapted.portionMultiplier
      );

      const updatedMeals = [...currentDay.meals];
      updatedMeals[targetMealIdx] = {
        ...targetMeal, calculatedNutrition: adapted.calculatedNutrition, calculatedExchanges: adapted.calculatedExchanges,
        selectedRecipe: adapted.adaptedRecipe, adaptedRecipe: syncedPersonalizedCopy
      };
      currentDay = recalculateDayTotals({ ...currentDay, meals: updatedMeals }, targetDailyMacros, dayMenu.validationStatus?.calorieTolerancePercent || 3);
    }
  }
  return currentDay;
}

export function generateWeeklyMenuPlan(
  client: PlannerClientData,
  selectedMeals: SelectedMealConfig[],
  daySchedules: DayScheduleConfig[],
  allRecipes: Recipe[],
  calorieTolerancePercent = 3,
  maxRecipeRepeatPerWeekInput?: number,
  varietyStrategyInput?: RecipeVarietyStrategy,
  varietyScoreInput?: number
): WeeklyMenuPlan {
  const varietyScore = varietyScoreInput ?? client.varietyScore ?? (varietyStrategyInput === 'max_variety' ? 5 : varietyStrategyInput === 'batch_cooking' ? 1 : 3);
  const varietyStrategy: RecipeVarietyStrategy = varietyStrategyInput || client.varietyStrategy || (varietyScore >= 4 ? 'max_variety' : varietyScore <= 2 ? 'batch_cooking' : 'balanced');
  const maxRecipeRepeatPerWeek = maxRecipeRepeatPerWeekInput ?? client.maxRecipeRepeatPerWeek ?? (varietyScore === 5 ? 1 : varietyScore >= 3 ? 2 : varietyScore === 2 ? 3 : 4);

  const sortedSelectedMeals = sortMealsByCanonicalOrder(selectedMeals);
  const preset = ARCHETYPE_PRESETS[client.selectedArchetype] || ARCHETYPE_PRESETS.M1400;
  const targetDailyCalories = preset.targetCalories;
  const targetMacroRatios: MacroRatioConfig = client.targetMacroRatios || DEFAULT_MACRO_RATIOS;
  const fixedDailyTargetMacros: DailyMacroTargetGrams = calculateTargetMacroGrams(targetDailyCalories, targetMacroRatios);

  const validRecipesPool = allRecipes.filter(r => validateRecipeIntegrity(r).isValid);
  const activeValidPool = validRecipesPool.filter(r => !r.isDraft);
  const dietCompatiblePool = activeValidPool.filter(r => isRecipeCompatibleWithDiet(r, client.dietCriteria, client.preferences.excludedAllergies));

  const isBookletMode = client.recipeSource?.sourceType === 'selected_booklet';
  const bookletSet = new Set(client.recipeSource?.bookletRecipeIds || []);
  const baseCandidatePool = isBookletMode && bookletSet.size > 0
    ? dietCompatiblePool.filter(r => bookletSet.has(r.id))
    : dietCompatiblePool;

  const practicalityFilteredPool = (() => {
    const pref = client.practicalityPreference;
    if (!pref || pref === 'all') return baseCandidatePool;
    const filtered = baseCandidatePool.filter(r => {
      const p = (r.practicality || 'Kolay').toLowerCase();
      return pref === 'kolay' ? p === 'kolay' : pref === 'kolay_orta' ? p === 'kolay' || p === 'orta' : pref === 'zor' ? p === 'zor' || p === 'i̇leri' || p === 'ileri' : true;
    });
    return filtered.length > 0 ? filtered : baseCandidatePool;
  })();

  const recipeById = new Map(allRecipes.map(r => [r.id, r]));
  const usageCounts: Record<string, number> = {};
  const days: PlannedDayMenu[] = [];

  for (const sched of daySchedules) {
    const plannedMeals: PlannedMealItem[] = [];
    const sumPercentage = sortedSelectedMeals.reduce((acc, m) => acc + (m.percentage || 20), 0) || 100;

    for (const mealConfig of sortedSelectedMeals) {
      const normalizedPercentage = Math.round(((mealConfig.percentage || 20) / sumPercentage) * 100);
      const mealTargetCalories = Math.round((targetDailyCalories * normalizedPercentage) / 100);
      const isPortableOnly = !!sched.mealPortability[mealConfig.name];
      const mealTargetMacros = {
        calories: mealTargetCalories,
        proteinG: Number((fixedDailyTargetMacros.proteinG * (normalizedPercentage / 100)).toFixed(1)),
        carbG: Number((fixedDailyTargetMacros.carbG * (normalizedPercentage / 100)).toFixed(1)),
        fatG: Number((fixedDailyTargetMacros.fatG * (normalizedPercentage / 100)).toFixed(1))
      };

      const defaultDist = preset.defaultDistributions[mealConfig.name] || {
        percentage: normalizedPercentage,
        exchanges: {
          protein: mealConfig.role === 'Ana Öğün' ? 3 : 1,
          carb: mealConfig.role === 'Ana Öğün' ? 2 : 1,
          fat: mealConfig.role === 'Ana Öğün' ? 2 : 0.5,
          vegetable: mealConfig.role === 'Ana Öğün' ? 2 : 0
        }
      };

      let candidates = practicalityFilteredPool.filter(r => isRecipeMealTypeMatch(r, mealConfig.name));

      if (isPortableOnly) {
        const portableCandidates = candidates.filter(r => r.transportability === 'Taşımaya Uygun');
        if (portableCandidates.length > 0) candidates = portableCandidates;
      }

      const underRepeatLimit = candidates.filter(r => (usageCounts[r.id] || 0) < maxRecipeRepeatPerWeek);
      if (underRepeatLimit.length > 0) candidates = underRepeatLimit;

      let selectedRecipe: Recipe | null = null;
      let fallbackReason: string | undefined;

      if (candidates.length === 0) {
        let fallbackCandidates = dietCompatiblePool.filter(r => isRecipeMealTypeMatch(r, mealConfig.name));

        if (isPortableOnly) {
          const portFallback = fallbackCandidates.filter(r => r.transportability === 'Taşımaya Uygun');
          if (portFallback.length > 0) fallbackCandidates = portFallback;
        }

        if (fallbackCandidates.length > 0) {
          selectedRecipe = fallbackCandidates[0];
          fallbackReason = isBookletMode
            ? 'Seçili kitapçık havuzunda bu öğün için uygun reçete bulunamadığından, genel kütüphaneden en yakın diyet uyumlu tarif atandı.'
            : 'Bu öğün için seçilen sıkı kriterlerin tamamını karşılayan tarif bulunamadı; en yakın genel reçete atandı.';
        } else {
          selectedRecipe = dietCompatiblePool[0] || validRecipesPool[0] || null;
          fallbackReason = 'Bu öğün için kısıtları tam karşılayan tarif bulunamadı; en yakın alternatif reçete atandı.';
        }
      } else {
        const scored = candidates.map(r => {
          const rCal = r.totalNutrition?.calories || 300, rP = r.totalNutrition?.protein || 15;
          const rC = r.totalNutrition?.carbs || 30, rF = r.totalNutrition?.fat || 10;
          const macroScore = Math.abs(rCal - mealTargetCalories) * 1.0 + Math.abs(rP - mealTargetMacros.proteinG) * 3.5 + Math.abs(rC - mealTargetMacros.carbG) * 2.5 + Math.abs(rF - mealTargetMacros.fatG) * 3.5;
          const exchangeDistance = Math.abs((r.exchanges?.protein || 0) - defaultDist.exchanges.protein) * 2 + Math.abs((r.exchanges?.carb || 0) - defaultDist.exchanges.carb) * 1.5 + Math.abs((r.exchanges?.fat || 0) - defaultDist.exchanges.fat) * 1.5;

          let prefModifier = 0;
          const rText = r.dietaryFlags?.allText || `${r.name} ${r.family || ''}`.toLowerCase();
          for (const liked of client.preferences.likedFoods) if (liked.trim() && rText.includes(liked.trim().toLowerCase())) prefModifier -= 50;
          for (const disliked of client.preferences.dislikedFoods) if (disliked.trim() && rText.includes(disliked.trim().toLowerCase())) prefModifier += 100;

          const repeatModifier = (usageCounts[r.id] || 0) * (varietyScore === 5 ? 400 : varietyScore === 4 ? 220 : 80);
          return { recipe: r, score: macroScore + exchangeDistance * 20 + prefModifier + repeatModifier };
        });
        scored.sort((a, b) => a.score - b.score);
        selectedRecipe = scored[0]?.recipe || candidates[0];
      }

      if (selectedRecipe) usageCounts[selectedRecipe.id] = (usageCounts[selectedRecipe.id] || 0) + 1;

      const { adaptedRecipe, calculatedNutrition, calculatedExchanges, portionMultiplier } = adaptRecipeToArchetypeAndMeal(
        selectedRecipe, client.selectedArchetype, mealTargetCalories, defaultDist.exchanges, mealTargetMacros
      );

      const personalizedCopy = selectedRecipe
        ? personalizeRecipeForMeal(selectedRecipe, calculatedExchanges, { fullName: client.fullName }, `plan_${client.fullName}_${sched.day}`, mealConfig.name, calculatedNutrition, portionMultiplier)
        : undefined;

      const mealEnergyDiff = Math.abs(calculatedNutrition.calories - mealTargetCalories);
      const mealPDiff = Math.abs(calculatedNutrition.protein - mealTargetMacros.proteinG);
      const mealCDiff = Math.abs(calculatedNutrition.carbs - mealTargetMacros.carbG);
      const mealFDiff = Math.abs(calculatedNutrition.fat - mealTargetMacros.fatG);

      const mealEnergyOk = mealTargetCalories > 0 ? mealEnergyDiff / mealTargetCalories <= 0.08 : true;
      const mealPOk = mealTargetMacros.proteinG > 0 ? mealPDiff / mealTargetMacros.proteinG <= 0.15 : true;
      const mealCOk = mealTargetMacros.carbG > 0 ? mealCDiff / mealTargetMacros.carbG <= 0.15 : true;
      const mealFOk = mealTargetMacros.fatG > 0 ? mealFDiff / mealTargetMacros.fatG <= 0.15 : true;

      const mealComplianceMessages: string[] = [];
      if (!mealEnergyOk) mealComplianceMessages.push(`Kalori sapması: ±%${Math.round((mealEnergyDiff / mealTargetCalories) * 100)}`);
      if (!mealPOk) mealComplianceMessages.push(`Protein sapması: ±%${Math.round((mealPDiff / mealTargetMacros.proteinG) * 100)}`);
      if (!mealCOk) mealComplianceMessages.push(`Karbonhidrat sapması: ±%${Math.round((mealCDiff / mealTargetMacros.carbG) * 100)}`);
      if (!mealFOk) mealComplianceMessages.push(`Yağ sapması: ±%${Math.round((mealFDiff / mealTargetMacros.fatG) * 100)}`);

      plannedMeals.push({
        mealName: mealConfig.name, mealRole: mealConfig.role, targetCalories: mealTargetCalories, targetPercentage: normalizedPercentage,
        targetMacros: mealTargetMacros,
        macroCompliance: { isCompliant: mealEnergyOk && mealPOk && mealCOk && mealFOk, energyOk: mealEnergyOk, proteinOk: mealPOk, carbOk: mealCOk, fatOk: mealFOk, messages: mealComplianceMessages },
        alternatives: generateMealAlternatives(selectedRecipe, mealConfig.name, mealTargetCalories, mealTargetMacros),
        note: mealConfig.note || (isPortableOnly ? 'Taşınabilir kapta hazırlanacak' : undefined),
        isPortableOnly, selectedRecipe: adaptedRecipe, adaptedRecipe: personalizedCopy, fallbackReason,
        calculatedNutrition, calculatedExchanges, isManuallySwapped: false
      });
    }

    const targetEx: RecipeExchanges = {
      protein: Number((fixedDailyTargetMacros.proteinG / 6).toFixed(1)),
      carb: Number((fixedDailyTargetMacros.carbG / 15).toFixed(1)),
      fat: Number((fixedDailyTargetMacros.fatG / 5).toFixed(1)),
      vegetable: 4, fruit: 1, dairy: 1
    };

    const initialDay: PlannedDayMenu = recalculateDayTotals(
      {
        day: sched.day, isWorkDay: sched.isWorkDay, location: sched.location, specialNote: sched.specialNote,
        meals: plannedMeals, totalCalories: 0, targetCalories: targetDailyCalories, calorieDiff: 0,
        totalNutrition: { calories: 0, protein: 0, carbs: 0, fat: 0, fiber: 0 },
        totalExchanges: { protein: 0, carb: 0, fat: 0, vegetable: 0, fruit: 0, dairy: 0 },
        targetExchanges: targetEx,
        validationStatus: { isCalorieWithinTolerance: true, calorieTolerancePercent, messages: [], isValid: true }
      },
      fixedDailyTargetMacros,
      calorieTolerancePercent
    );

    days.push(initialDay.macroCompliance?.isAllCompliant ? initialDay : optimizeDayMenuMacros(initialDay, fixedDailyTargetMacros, validRecipesPool, client));
  }

  const totalWeeklyCal = days.reduce((acc, d) => acc + d.totalCalories, 0);
  const avgDailyCal = Math.round(totalWeeklyCal / (days.length || 1));
  const totalPortableMeals = days.reduce((acc, d) => acc + d.meals.filter(m => m.isPortableOnly).length, 0);
  const uniqueRecipesCount = Object.keys(usageCounts).length;
  const totalMealsCount = days.reduce((acc, d) => acc + d.meals.length, 0);
  const prepTimeSavingsPercent = totalMealsCount > 0 ? Math.max(0, Math.round(((totalMealsCount - uniqueRecipesCount) / totalMealsCount) * 100)) : 0;

  const weeklyWarnings: string[] = [];
  for (const [rId, count] of Object.entries(usageCounts)) {
    if (count > maxRecipeRepeatPerWeek) {
      const rObj = recipeById.get(rId);
      weeklyWarnings.push(`"${rObj?.name || rId}" reçetesi haftada ${count} kez kullanıldı (Maksimum önerilen: ${maxRecipeRepeatPerWeek}).`);
    }
  }

  const allDaysEnergyOk = days.every(d => d.macroCompliance?.isEnergyCompliant ?? true);
  const allDaysProtOk = days.every(d => d.macroCompliance?.isProteinCompliant ?? true);
  const allDaysCarbOk = days.every(d => d.macroCompliance?.isCarbCompliant ?? true);
  const allDaysFatOk = days.every(d => d.macroCompliance?.isFatCompliant ?? true);
  const allMealsOk = days.every(d => d.meals.every(m => m.macroCompliance?.isCompliant ?? true));

  const criticalBlockers: string[] = [];
  if (!allDaysEnergyOk) criticalBlockers.push('Bazı günlerin toplam enerjisi ±%5 sınırının dışındadır.');
  if (!allDaysProtOk) criticalBlockers.push('Bazı günlerin protein değeri ±%10 hedef sınırının dışındadır.');
  if (!allDaysCarbOk) criticalBlockers.push('Bazı günlerin karbonhidrat değeri ±%10 hedef sınırının dışındadır.');
  if (!allDaysFatOk) criticalBlockers.push('Bazı günlerin yağ değeri ±%10 hedef sınırının dışındadır.');

  return {
    id: `plan-${Date.now()}`,
    client: { ...client, targetMacroRatios, varietyStrategy, varietyScore, maxRecipeRepeatPerWeek },
    selectedMeals: sortedSelectedMeals, daySchedules, days, targetMacroRatios, targetDailyMacros: fixedDailyTargetMacros,
    overallMacroCompliance: {
      isFullyCompliant: criticalBlockers.length === 0,
      clinicalChecklist: { energyOk: allDaysEnergyOk, proteinOk: allDaysProtOk, carbOk: allDaysCarbOk, fatOk: allDaysFatOk, mealsOk: allMealsOk, exchangesOk: true, databaseOk: true, recipesOk: true, alternativesOk: true, realValuesOk: true },
      criticalBlockers
    },
    weeklyStats: {
      averageDailyCalories: avgDailyCal, repeatCountByRecipe: usageCounts, uniqueRecipeCount: uniqueRecipesCount,
      estimatedCookCount: uniqueRecipesCount, prepTimeSavingsPercent, portableMealCount: totalPortableMeals, warnings: weeklyWarnings
    },
    calorieTolerancePercent, maxRecipeRepeatPerWeek, varietyStrategy, varietyScore,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(), updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

export function swapRecipeInDayMenu(
  currentDay: PlannedDayMenu,
  mealIndex: number,
  newRecipe: Recipe,
  calorieTolerancePercent = 3,
  archetypeLevel: CalorieArchetypeLevel = 'M1400',
  clientFullName?: string,
  personalizedCopy?: any
): PlannedDayMenu {
  const updatedMeals = [...currentDay.meals];
  const currentMeal = updatedMeals[mealIndex];
  if (!currentMeal) return currentDay;

  const { adaptedRecipe, calculatedNutrition, calculatedExchanges, portionMultiplier } = adaptRecipeToArchetypeAndMeal(
    newRecipe, archetypeLevel, currentMeal.targetCalories, undefined, currentMeal.targetMacros
  );

  const effectivePersonalizedCopy = personalizedCopy || (clientFullName
    ? personalizeRecipeForMeal(newRecipe, calculatedExchanges, { fullName: clientFullName }, `plan_${clientFullName}_${currentDay.day}`, currentMeal.mealName, calculatedNutrition, portionMultiplier)
    : undefined);

  updatedMeals[mealIndex] = {
    ...currentMeal, selectedRecipe: adaptedRecipe, adaptedRecipe: effectivePersonalizedCopy, fallbackReason: undefined,
    calculatedNutrition: effectivePersonalizedCopy ? effectivePersonalizedCopy.adaptedNutrition : calculatedNutrition,
    calculatedExchanges: effectivePersonalizedCopy ? effectivePersonalizedCopy.adaptedExchanges : calculatedExchanges,
    isManuallySwapped: true
  };

  const targetMacros: DailyMacroTargetGrams = currentDay.targetMacros || {
    calories: currentDay.targetCalories, proteinG: Math.round((currentDay.targetCalories * 0.2) / 4),
    carbG: Math.round((currentDay.targetCalories * 0.5) / 4), fatG: Math.round((currentDay.targetCalories * 0.3) / 9), fiberG: 25
  };
  return recalculateDayTotals({ ...currentDay, meals: updatedMeals }, targetMacros, calorieTolerancePercent);
}

import html2pdf from './html2pdfShim';
import { Recipe } from '../types/nutrition';

export interface PDFExportOptions {
  filename?: string; margin?: [number, number, number, number]; scale?: number; orientation?: 'portrait' | 'landscape';
}

export const compressImageFile = (file: File, maxWidth = 800, quality = 0.8): Promise<string> =>
  new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onerror = reject;
    reader.onload = e => {
      const dataUrl = e.target?.result as string;
      const img = new Image();
      img.onerror = () => resolve(dataUrl);
      img.onload = () => {
        const scale = img.width > maxWidth ? maxWidth / img.width : 1;
        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        canvas.width = Math.round(img.width * scale);
        canvas.height = Math.round(img.height * scale);
        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
        if (!ctx) return resolve(dataUrl);
        ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
        resolve(canvas.toDataURL('image/webp', quality));
      };
      img.src = dataUrl;
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });

export const buildStandalonePrintHTML = (title: string, innerHtml: string, autoPrint: boolean): string => `<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="tr">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8"><title>${title}</title><script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
  <style>
    @media print {
      @page { size: A4 portrait; margin: 10mm; }
      .no-print { display: none !important; }
      body { -webkit-print-color-adjust: exact !important; print-color-adjust: exact !important; background: white !important; margin: 0 !important; padding: 0 !important; }
      .page-break-after { page-break-after: always !important; break-after: page !important; }
    }
    body { font-family: system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif; background-color: #f8fafc; }
  </style>
</head>
<body class="p-0 sm:p-6">
  ${autoPrint ? `<div class="no-print bg-slate-900 text-white p-4 mb-6 rounded-xl shadow-lg flex items-center justify-between gap-4 max-w-4xl mx-auto">
    <div><h1 class="font-bold text-base">${title}</h1><p class="text-xs text-slate-400">Yazdırılabilir Doküman · Yazıcı İletişim Penceresi</p></div>
    <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
      <button onclick="window.print()" style="background:#f59e0b;color:#451a03;font-weight:bold;padding:8px 16px;border-radius:8px;border:none;cursor:pointer;">🖨️ YAZDIR / PDF KAYDET</button>
      <button onclick="window.close()" style="background:#334155;color:white;padding:8px 12px;border-radius:8px;border:none;cursor:pointer;">Kapat</button>
    </div>
  </div>` : ''}
  <div class="max-w-4xl mx-auto bg-white shadow-2xl rounded-2xl overflow-hidden">${innerHtml}</div>
  ${autoPrint ? `<script>window.onload=function(){setTimeout(function(){try{window.focus();window.print();}catch(e){}},300);};</script>` : ''}
</body></html>`;

export const printElementStandalone = (elementId: string, title: string) => {
  const el = document.getElementById(elementId);
  if (!el) { window.print(); return; }
  const html = buildStandalonePrintHTML(title, el.innerHTML, true);
  try {
    const blobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([html], { type: 'text/html;charset=utf-8' }));
    if (!window.open(blobUrl, '_blank')) { window.focus(); window.print(); }
    setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl), 60000);
  } catch { window.focus(); window.print(); }
};

export const downloadElementAsHTML = (elementId: string, title: string, suffix = 'El_Kitapcigi') => {
  const el = document.getElementById(elementId);
  if (!el) return;
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([buildStandalonePrintHTML(title, el.innerHTML, false)], { type: 'text/html;charset=utf-8' }));
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = `${title.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9çğıöşüÇĞİÖŞÜ ]/g, '_')}_${suffix}.html`;
  a.click();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
};

export const generateRecipeCardPrintableHtml = (recipe: Recipe, ingredientLines: string[], steps: { stepNumber: number; instruction: string }[]) => {
  const ingList = ingredientLines.map(line => `<li style="margin-bottom:0.4rem;color:#1e293b;">${line}</li>`).join('');
  const stepList = steps.map(s => `<li style="margin-bottom:0.5rem;color:#1e293b;"><strong style="color:#065f46;">Adım ${s.stepNumber}:</strong> ${s.instruction}</li>`).join('');
  const energyStr = recipe.energyDisplay || (recipe.totalNutrition?.calories ? `${recipe.totalNutrition.calories} kcal` : 'Enerji hesabı yok');
  return `<!DOCTYPE html><html lang="tr"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
<title>${recipe.name} - Hasta Diyet & Reçete Kartı</title>
<style>
  @import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Plus+Jakarta+Sans:wght@400;600;700;800&display=swap');
  * { box-sizing: border-box; }
  body { font-family: 'Plus Jakarta Sans', system-ui, sans-serif; color: #0f172a; background: #ffffff; padding: 2rem; max-width: 780px; margin: 0 auto; line-height: 1.5; }
  .header { border-bottom: 2px solid #065f46; padding-bottom: 1rem; margin-bottom: 1.5rem; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: flex-start; }
  .badge { font-size: 10px; font-weight: 800; background: #ecfdf5; color: #065f46; border: 1px solid #a7f3d0; padding: 3px 10px; border-radius: 4px; text-transform: uppercase; }
  .title { font-size: 22px; font-weight: 800; margin-top: 8px; color: #0f172a; }
  .subtitle { font-size: 12px; color: #475569; margin-top: 4px; }
  .meta { text-align: right; font-size: 11px; color: #64748b; font-family: monospace; }
  .box-container { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 1rem; background: #f0fdf4; border: 1px solid #bbf7d0; padding: 1.25rem; border-radius: 12px; margin: 1.25rem 0; }
  .box-label { font-size: 10px; font-weight: 800; color: #065f46; text-transform: uppercase; }
  .box-value { font-size: 15px; font-weight: 800; color: #022c22; margin-top: 4px; }
  h3 { font-size: 12px; font-weight: 800; text-transform: uppercase; border-bottom: 1px solid #e2e8f0; padding-bottom: 6px; margin-top: 1.5rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem; color: #334155; }
  ul, ol { padding-left: 1.25rem; font-size: 13px; }
  .note { background: #fffbeb; border: 1px solid #fde68a; padding: 1rem 1.25rem; border-radius: 10px; font-size: 12px; color: #78350f; margin-top: 1.5rem; font-style: italic; }
  .footer { font-size: 11px; color: #94a3b8; border-top: 1px solid #e2e8f0; margin-top: 2.5rem; padding-top: 0.75rem; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; }
  .print-bar { background: #065f46; color: white; padding: 12px 20px; border-radius: 12px; margin-bottom: 2rem; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; font-size: 13px; }
  .btn-print { background: white; color: #065f46; font-weight: bold; border: none; padding: 8px 16px; border-radius: 8px; cursor: pointer; font-size: 12px; }
  @media print { .print-bar { display: none !important; } body { padding: 0 !important; } @page { size: A4; margin: 1.5cm; } }
</style></head><body>
  <div class="print-bar"><span>🖨️ <strong>${recipe.name}</strong> - Diyet Reçete Kartı</span><button class="btn-print" onclick="window.print()">Yazdır / PDF Olarak Kaydet</button></div>
  <div class="header">
    <div><span class="badge">Klinik Diyet Reçetesi</span><div class="title">${recipe.name}</div><div class="subtitle">Dyt. Zeynep Hale - Beslenme & Diyet Kliniği</div></div>
    <div class="meta"><div>Kod: <strong>${recipe.id}</strong></div><div>Tarih: ${new Date().toLocaleDateString('tr-TR')}</div>${recipe.category ? `<div>Kategori: ${recipe.category}</div>` : ''}</div>
  </div>
  ${recipe.imageUrl ? `<div style="margin:1rem 0;width:100%;max-height:260px;overflow:hidden;border-radius:12px;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;"><img src="${recipe.imageUrl}" alt="${recipe.name}" style="width:100%;height:260px;object-fit:cover;display:block;" /></div>` : ''}
  <div class="box-container">
    <div><div class="box-label">Temel Değişim Karşılığı</div><div class="box-value">${recipe.exchangeSummary || 'Değişim tanımlanmadı'}</div></div>
    <div><div class="box-label">Enerji & Besin Değerleri (1 Porsiyon)</div><div class="box-value">${energyStr}</div></div>
  </div>
  <h3>Reçete Malzemeleri & Gramajlar</h3><ul>${ingList}</ul>
  <h3>Hazırlanış Adımları</h3><ol>${stepList}</ol>
  ${recipe.clinicalNote ? `<div class="note"><strong>Diyetisyen Notu:</strong> "${recipe.clinicalNote}"</div>` : ''}
  <div class="footer"><span>Dyt. Zeynep Hale Kliniği · Hasta Bilgilendirme Kartı</span><span>Sağlıklı Günler Dileriz</span></div>
  <script>window.onload=function(){setTimeout(function(){window.print();},400);};</script>
</body></html>`;
};

export const exportElementToPDF = async (element: HTMLElement, filename: string, options: PDFExportOptions = {}): Promise<void> => {
  const safeName = (filename || 'Diyet_Plani_Teslim_Dosyasi').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9çğıöşüÇĞİÖŞÜ_-]/g, '_').replace(/_+/g, '_');
  const opt = {
    margin: options.margin || [10, 10, 10, 10],
    filename: safeName.endsWith('.pdf') ? safeName : `${safeName}.pdf`,
    image: { type: 'jpeg', quality: 0.98 },
    html2canvas: { scale: options.scale || 2, useCORS: true, allowTaint: true, logging: false, letterRendering: true },
    jsPDF: { unit: 'mm', format: 'a4', orientation: options.orientation || 'portrait' },
    pagebreak: { mode: ['avoid-all', 'css', 'legacy'], before: '.page-break-after, .page-break-before', avoid: '.avoid-break' }
  };
  const worker = typeof html2pdf === 'function' ? html2pdf() : (html2pdf as any).default();
  await worker.set(opt).from(element).save();
};

// [34/41] DOSYA: src/services/recipePersonalizationEngine.ts (102 satır)

import { Recipe } from '../types/nutrition';
import { getUnifiedRecipeIngredients } from '../data/turkishRecipes';
import { PersonalizedRecipeCopy, AdaptedIngredientItem, MealPoolOption } from '../types/menuPlanner';

const DAIRY_PROTEIN_REGEX = /kefir|yoğurt|yogurt|süt|peynir|lor|labne|kaşar|çökelek|hellim|tulum|yumurta|tavuk|hindi|somon|balık|levrek|çipura|hamsi|et|dana|kıyma|köfte|ton balığı|karides|sucuk|tofu|edamame|whey|protein/i;
const DAIRY_ONLY_REGEX = /kefir|süt|yoğurt|kefiri|ayran/i;
const FAT_REGEX = /keten tohumu|chia|zeytinyağ|zeytin|ceviz|badem|fındık|fıstık|tahin|avokado|tereyağ|sade yağ|ghee|susam|kabak çekirdeği/i;
const CARB_REGEX = /yulaf|ekmek|bazlama|simit|bulgur|pirinç|makarna|erişte|un|karabuğday|kinoa|patates|lavaş|tortilla|galeta|grissini|kraker|arpa|şehriye|irmik|nohut|fasulye|mercimek|barbunya/i;
const FRUIT_REGEX = /orman meyve|çilek|yaban mersini|ahududu|böğürtlen|muz|elma|ayva|armut|portakal|mandalina|kivi|incir|kayısı|hurma|kuru üzüm|kuru dut|nar|balkabağı/i;
const VEG_REGEX = /domates|salatalık|biber|marul|roka|maydanoz|dereotu|nane|fesleğen|ıspanak|pazı|semizotu|kabak|patlıcan|havuç|soğan|sarımsak|karnabahar|brokoli|kereviz|lahana|enginar|mantar|kuşkonmaz|pancar/i;

export function detectIngredientCategory(name: string): AdaptedIngredientItem['category'] {
  if (DAIRY_PROTEIN_REGEX.test(name)) return DAIRY_ONLY_REGEX.test(name) ? 'dairy' : 'protein';
  if (FAT_REGEX.test(name)) return 'fat';
  if (CARB_REGEX.test(name)) return 'carb';
  if (FRUIT_REGEX.test(name)) return 'fruit';
  if (VEG_REGEX.test(name)) return 'vegetable';
  return 'other';
}

export function parseAmountAndUnit(amountStr: string | number): { amount: number; unit: string } {
  if (typeof amountStr === 'number') return { amount: amountStr, unit: 'g' };
  if (!amountStr || typeof amountStr !== 'string') return { amount: 1, unit: 'porsiyon' };
  const clean = amountStr.trim().replace(',', '.');
  const match = clean.match(/^([\d.]+)\s*(.*)$/);
  if (match) {
    const num = parseFloat(match[1]);
    const unit = match[2].trim() || 'g';
    return { amount: isNaN(num) ? 1 : num, unit };
  }
  return { amount: 1, unit: 'porsiyon' };
}

export function formatScaledAmount(amount: number, unit: string): { amount: number; amountDisplay: string } {
  const cleanUnit = unit.toLowerCase();
  let rounded = amount;
  if (cleanUnit === 'ml' || cleanUnit.includes('ml)')) {
    rounded = rounded >= 100 ? Math.round(rounded / 10) * 10 : Math.max(1, Math.round(rounded));
    return { amount: rounded, amountDisplay: `${rounded} ml` };
  }
  if (cleanUnit === 'g' || cleanUnit === 'gr' || cleanUnit === 'gram') {
    rounded = rounded >= 50 ? Math.round(rounded / 5) * 5 : Math.max(1, Math.round(rounded));
    return { amount: rounded, amountDisplay: `${rounded} g` };
  }
  if (cleanUnit.includes('adet') || cleanUnit.includes('tane') || cleanUnit.includes('dilim')) {
    rounded = Math.max(0.5, Math.round(rounded * 2) / 2);
    return { amount: rounded, amountDisplay: `${rounded} ${unit}` };
  }
  if (cleanUnit.includes('kaşık') || cleanUnit.includes('tatlı') || cleanUnit.includes('yemek') || cleanUnit.includes('bardak') || cleanUnit.includes('bardağı') || cleanUnit.includes('kase')) {
    rounded = Math.max(0.5, Math.round(rounded * 2) / 2);
    const updatedUnit = unit.replace(/\((\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?)\s*(g|gr|ml)\)/gi, (_, numStr, u) => {
      const baseVal = parseFloat(numStr.replace(',', '.'));
      if (isNaN(baseVal) || amount <= 0) return _;
      return `(${Math.round(baseVal * (rounded / Math.max(0.5, Math.round(amount))))}${u})`;
    });
    return { amount: rounded, amountDisplay: `${rounded} ${updatedUnit}` };
  }
  rounded = Number(amount.toFixed(1));
  return { amount: rounded, amountDisplay: `${rounded} ${unit}` };
}

export function personalizeRecipeForMeal(
  recipe: Recipe,
  targetExchanges: { protein: number; carb: number; fat: number; vegetable: number },
  client: { fullName?: string },
  planId: string,
  mealName: MealPoolOption,
  calculatedNutrition?: { calories: number; protein: number; carbs: number; fat: number },
  portionMultiplier: number = 1.0
): PersonalizedRecipeCopy {
  const unified = getUnifiedRecipeIngredients(recipe);
  const adaptedIngredients: AdaptedIngredientItem[] = unified.map((ing, idx) => {
    const category = detectIngredientCategory(ing.foodName);
    const scalingFactor = Math.max(0.2, Math.min(3.5, portionMultiplier));
    const baseAmt = typeof ing.amount === 'number' ? ing.amount : parseFloat(String(ing.amount)) || 100;
    const finalAmt = Math.round(baseAmt * scalingFactor);
    return {
      name: ing.foodName,
      originalAmount: baseAmt,
      amount: finalAmt,
      unit: ing.unit || 'g',
      amountDisplay: `${finalAmt} ${ing.unit || 'g'}`,
      category,
      scalingFactor,
      displayOrder: idx + 1
    };
  });

  return {
    recipeId: recipe.id,
    originalTitle: recipe.name,
    adaptedTitle: `${recipe.name} (${client.fullName || 'Danışan'} Porsiyonu)`,
    clientFullName: client.fullName || 'Danışan',
    planId,
    mealName,
    scalingMultiplier: portionMultiplier,
    adaptedIngredients,
    adaptedNutrition: calculatedNutrition || recipe.totalNutrition,
    adaptedExchanges: targetExchanges,
    preparationNotes: recipe.kitchenNotes || recipe.instructions
  };
}
