// [35/41] DOSYA: src/services/turKompDatabaseService.ts (145 satır)
================================================================================

import { FoodItem, RecipeIngredient, RecipeTotalNutrition, RecipeExchanges, Recipe, FoodSource } from '../types/nutrition';
import { INITIAL_FOODS } from '../data/mockFoods';
import { computeRecipeDietaryFlags } from '../data/turkishRecipes';
import { calculateIngredientNutrition, estimateExchangesFromIngredients, calculateRecipeTotalNutrition } from '../utils/nutritionCalculator';

export interface TurKompDatabaseStats {
  totalFoods: number; turKompCount: number; usdaCount: number; categoriesCount: number;
  version: string; sourceAuthority: string; deterministicMode: boolean;
}

export interface IngredientCalculationResult {
  grams: number; calories: number; protein: number; carbs: number; fat: number; fiber: number;
}

export class TurKompDatabaseService {
  private foodList: FoodItem[];
  private foodMapById = new Map<string, FoodItem>();
  private foodMapByCode = new Map<string, FoodItem>();
  private foodMapByNameLower = new Map<string, FoodItem>();

  constructor(initialFoods: FoodItem[] = INITIAL_FOODS) {
    this.foodList = [...initialFoods];
    this.indexFoods();
  }

  private indexFoods() {
    this.foodMapById.clear();
    this.foodMapByCode.clear();
    this.foodMapByNameLower.clear();
    for (const food of this.foodList) {
      if (food.id) this.foodMapById.set(food.id, food);
      if (food.foodCode) this.foodMapByCode.set(food.foodCode.toUpperCase(), food);
      if (food.name) this.foodMapByNameLower.set(food.name.toLowerCase().trim(), food);
    }
  }

  public getAllFoods(): FoodItem[] { return [...this.foodList]; }

  public getFood(identifier: string): FoodItem | undefined {
    if (!identifier) return undefined;
    const cleanId = identifier.trim();
    if (this.foodMapById.has(cleanId)) return this.foodMapById.get(cleanId);
    const upperCode = cleanId.toUpperCase();
    if (this.foodMapByCode.has(upperCode)) return this.foodMapByCode.get(upperCode);
    const lowerName = cleanId.toLowerCase();
    if (this.foodMapByNameLower.has(lowerName)) return this.foodMapByNameLower.get(lowerName);
    return this.foodList.find(f => f.name.toLowerCase().includes(lowerName) || f.id.includes(lowerName));
  }

  public searchFoods(query: string, sourceFilter: 'Tümü' | 'TürKomp' | 'USDA' = 'Tümü', categoryFilter = 'Tümü'): FoodItem[] {
    const q = query.toLowerCase().trim();
    return this.foodList.filter(food => {
      if (sourceFilter === 'TürKomp' && !(food.source === 'TürKomp' || (!food.source && food.foodCode?.startsWith('TK')))) return false;
      if (sourceFilter === 'USDA' && food.source !== 'USDA') return false;
      if (categoryFilter !== 'Tümü' && food.category !== categoryFilter) return false;
      if (!q) return true;
      return (
        food.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || food.foodCode?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        food.latinName?.toLowerCase().includes(q) || food.category.toLowerCase().includes(q) || food.notes?.toLowerCase().includes(q)
      );
    });
  }

  public calculateIngredient(foodOrId: FoodItem | string, amount: number, unit: string): IngredientCalculationResult {
    const food = typeof foodOrId === 'string' ? this.getFood(foodOrId) : foodOrId;
    if (!food) return { grams: 0, calories: 0, protein: 0, carbs: 0, fat: 0, fiber: 0 };
    return calculateIngredientNutrition(food, amount, unit);
  }

  public calculateRecipeTotals(ingredients: RecipeIngredient[]): {
    totalNutrition: RecipeTotalNutrition; exchanges: RecipeExchanges;
    exchangeSummary: string; energyDisplay: string; updatedIngredients: RecipeIngredient[];
  } {
    const updatedIngredients = ingredients.map(ing => {
      const food = this.getFood(ing.foodId) || this.getFood(ing.foodName);
      if (!food) return ing;
      const calc = this.calculateIngredient(food, ing.amount, ing.unit);
      return {
        ...ing, calculatedCalories: calc.calories, calculatedProtein: calc.protein,
        calculatedCarbs: calc.carbs, calculatedFat: calc.fat, calculatedFiber: calc.fiber
      };
    });

    const totalNutrition = calculateRecipeTotalNutrition(updatedIngredients);
    const exchanges = estimateExchangesFromIngredients(updatedIngredients, this.foodList);
    const exchangeParts: string[] = [];
    if (exchanges.protein > 0) exchangeParts.push(`${exchanges.protein} Et`);
    if (exchanges.carb > 0) exchangeParts.push(`${exchanges.carb} Tahıl`);
    if (exchanges.vegetable > 0) exchangeParts.push(`${exchanges.vegetable} Sebze`);
    if (exchanges.fruit > 0) exchangeParts.push(`${exchanges.fruit} Meyve`);
    if (exchanges.dairy > 0) exchangeParts.push(`${exchanges.dairy} Süt`);
    if (exchanges.fat > 0) exchangeParts.push(`${exchanges.fat} Yağ`);

    return {
      totalNutrition, exchanges,
      exchangeSummary: exchangeParts.length > 0 ? exchangeParts.join(' · ') : 'Serbest / Kalorisiz',
      energyDisplay: `${totalNutrition.calories} kcal (P: ${totalNutrition.protein}g · C: ${totalNutrition.carbs}g · Y: ${totalNutrition.fat}g)`,
      updatedIngredients
    };
  }

  public recalculateRecipe(recipe: Recipe): Recipe {
    if (!recipe.ingredients?.length) {
      return { ...recipe, dietaryFlags: recipe.dietaryFlags || computeRecipeDietaryFlags(recipe) };
    }
    const calculation = this.calculateRecipeTotals(recipe.ingredients);
    if (calculation.totalNutrition.calories === 0 && recipe.totalNutrition?.calories > 0) {
      return { ...recipe, dietaryFlags: recipe.dietaryFlags || computeRecipeDietaryFlags(recipe) };
    }
    const updated: Recipe = {
      ...recipe, ingredients: calculation.updatedIngredients, totalNutrition: calculation.totalNutrition,
      exchanges: calculation.exchanges, exchangeSummary: calculation.exchangeSummary,
      energyDisplay: calculation.energyDisplay, updatedAt: recipe.updatedAt || new Date().toISOString()
    };
    return { ...updated, dietaryFlags: computeRecipeDietaryFlags(updated) };
  }

  public addFood(food: FoodItem): FoodItem {
    const source: FoodSource = food.source || 'TürKomp';
    const foodCode = food.foodCode || (source === 'TürKomp' ? `TK-CS.${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}` : `USDA-${Date.now().toString().slice(-6)}`);
    const formattedFood: FoodItem = { ...food, source, foodCode };
    this.foodList = [formattedFood, ...this.foodList];
    this.indexFoods();
    return formattedFood;
  }

  public setFoods(foods: FoodItem[]) {
    this.foodList = [...foods];
    this.indexFoods();
  }

  public getStats(): TurKompDatabaseStats {
    const turKompCount = this.foodList.filter(f => f.source === 'TürKomp' || (!f.source && f.foodCode?.startsWith('TK'))).length;
    const usdaCount = this.foodList.filter(f => f.source === 'USDA').length;
    return {
      totalFoods: this.foodList.length, turKompCount, usdaCount,
      categoriesCount: new Set(this.foodList.map(f => f.category)).size,
      version: 'TürKomp v2.4 (Ulusal Gıda Kompozisyon Veri Tabanı) + USDA FDC Release',
      sourceAuthority: 'T.C. Tarım ve Orman Bakanlığı & TÜBİTAK MAM Ulusal Gıda Kompozisyon Veri Tabanı',
      deterministicMode: true
    };
  }
}

export const turKompMockService = new TurKompDatabaseService();

import { DietType, Recipe, RecipeExchanges, RecipeTotalNutrition } from './nutrition';

export type BMRFormula = 'mifflin_st_jeor' | 'harris_benedict';
export type CalorieArchetypeLevel = 'XS1000' | 'S1200' | 'M1400' | 'L1600' | 'XL1800';
export type MealPoolOption = 'Sabah' | 'Kahvaltı' | 'Kuşluk' | 'Öğle' | 'İkindi' | 'Akşam' | 'Gece';
export type MealRole = 'Ana Öğün' | 'Ara Öğün';
export type DayOfWeek = 'Pazartesi' | 'Salı' | 'Çarşamba' | 'Perşembe' | 'Cuma' | 'Cumartesi' | 'Pazar';
export type ActivityLevelOption =
  | 'Sedanter (Masa Başı)' | 'Sedanter (Masa Başı, Sıfır Egzersiz)' | 'Hafif Aktif (1-2 Gün Spor)'
  | 'Orta Düzey (3-4 Gün Spor)' | 'Orta Derece Aktif (3-5 Gün Spor)' | 'Yüksek Aktivite (5+ Gün)' | 'Çok Aktif (6-7 Gün Ağır Spor)';

export interface ArchetypePreset {
  level: CalorieArchetypeLevel; name: string; targetCalories: number; description: string; focus: string;
  defaultDistributions: Record<string, {
    percentage: number;
    exchanges: { protein: number; carb: number; fat: number; vegetable: number; fruit?: number; dairy?: number };
  }>;
}

export interface SelectedMealConfig { name: MealPoolOption; role: MealRole; percentage: number; note: string; }
export interface DayScheduleConfig {
  day: DayOfWeek; isWorkDay: boolean; location: 'Evde' | 'İşte / Dışarıda' | 'Hibrit';
  specialNote?: string; mealPortability: Record<MealPoolOption, boolean>;
}

export type RecipeSourceType = 'all_recipes' | 'selected_booklet';
export type RecipeVarietyStrategy = 'max_variety' | 'balanced' | 'batch_cooking' | 'custom';

export interface RecipeSourceConfig {
  sourceType: RecipeSourceType; bookletId?: string; bookletTitle?: string;
  bookletIds?: string[]; bookletTitles?: string[]; bookletRecipeIds?: string[];
}

export interface MacroRatioConfig { proteinPct: number; carbPct: number; fatPct: number; }
export interface DailyMacroTargetGrams { calories: number; proteinG: number; carbG: number; fatG: number; fiberG: number; }

export interface MacroComplianceResult {
  isEnergyCompliant: boolean; isProteinCompliant: boolean; isCarbCompliant: boolean;
  isFatCompliant: boolean; isAllCompliant: boolean; energyDiff: number; energyPctDiff: number;
  proteinDiff: number; proteinPctDiff: number; carbDiff: number; carbPctDiff: number;
  fatDiff: number; fatPctDiff: number; messages: string[]; needsReoptimization: boolean;
}

export interface MealAlternativeOption {
  id: string; name: string; type: 'equivalent' | 'macro_different'; calories: number;
  protein: number; carbs: number; fat: number; fiber: number; grams: number; warning?: string; isSelected?: boolean;
}

export interface PlannerClientData {
  clientId?: string; fullName: string; age: number; gender: 'Kadın' | 'Erkek';
  heightCm: number; weightKg: number; targetWeightKg?: number; activityLevel: ActivityLevelOption;
  bmrFormula: BMRFormula; calculatedBMR: number; calculatedBMI?: number; bmiCategory?: string;
  calculatedTargetEnergy: number; isEnergyBelowMinimumSafety: boolean; defaultArchetype: CalorieArchetypeLevel;
  selectedArchetype: CalorieArchetypeLevel; selectionMode: 'auto' | 'manual'; dietCriteria: DietType[];
  targetMacroRatios?: MacroRatioConfig; recipeSource?: RecipeSourceConfig;
  varietyStrategy?: RecipeVarietyStrategy; varietyScore?: number; maxRecipeRepeatPerWeek?: number;
  practicalityPreference?: 'all' | 'kolay' | 'kolay_orta' | 'zor';
  preferences: { likedFoods: string[]; dislikedFoods: string[]; excludedAllergies: string[] };
}

export interface AdaptedIngredientItem {
  name: string; originalAmountDisplay?: string; amount: number; unit: string; amountDisplay: string;
  category: 'protein' | 'carb' | 'fat' | 'fruit' | 'vegetable' | 'dairy' | 'other'; scalingFactor: number;
}

export interface PersonalizedRecipeCopy {
  recipeId: string; clientId: string; planId: string; mealId: string; mealName: MealPoolOption;
  baseRecipeName: string; adaptedTitle: string; portion: string; isAdaptedToTarget: boolean;
  adaptationNote: string; standardExchanges: RecipeExchanges; targetExchanges: RecipeExchanges;
  adaptedExchanges: RecipeExchanges; adaptedNutrition: RecipeTotalNutrition;
  adaptedIngredients: AdaptedIngredientItem[]; steps?: Recipe['steps']; prepTimeMinutes?: number;
  category?: string; imageUrl?: string; kitchenNotes?: string; clinicalNote?: string;
}

export interface PlannedMealItem {
  mealName: MealPoolOption; mealRole: MealRole; targetCalories: number; targetPercentage: number;
  targetMacros?: { calories: number; proteinG: number; carbG: number; fatG: number };
  macroCompliance?: { isCompliant: boolean; energyOk: boolean; proteinOk: boolean; carbOk: boolean; fatOk: boolean; messages: string[] };
  alternatives?: MealAlternativeOption[]; note?: string; isPortableOnly: boolean;
  selectedRecipe: Recipe | null; adaptedRecipe?: PersonalizedRecipeCopy; fallbackReason?: string;
  calculatedNutrition: RecipeTotalNutrition; calculatedExchanges: RecipeExchanges; isManuallySwapped?: boolean;
}

export interface PlannedDayMenu {
  day: DayOfWeek; isWorkDay: boolean; location: 'Evde' | 'İşte / Dışarıda' | 'Hibrit'; specialNote?: string;
  meals: PlannedMealItem[]; totalCalories: number; targetCalories: number; calorieDiff: number;
  totalNutrition: RecipeTotalNutrition; totalExchanges: RecipeExchanges; targetExchanges: RecipeExchanges;
  targetMacros?: DailyMacroTargetGrams; macroDiff?: { proteinG: number; carbG: number; fatG: number; fiberG: number };
  macroCompliance?: MacroComplianceResult;
  validationStatus: { isCalorieWithinTolerance: boolean; calorieTolerancePercent: number; messages: string[]; isValid: boolean };
}

export interface WeeklyMenuPlan {
  id: string; planTitle?: string; client: PlannerClientData; selectedMeals: SelectedMealConfig[];
  daySchedules: DayScheduleConfig[]; days: PlannedDayMenu[]; targetMacroRatios?: MacroRatioConfig;
  targetDailyMacros?: DailyMacroTargetGrams;
  overallMacroCompliance?: {
    isFullyCompliant: boolean;
    clinicalChecklist: {
      energyOk: boolean; proteinOk: boolean; carbOk: boolean; fatOk: boolean; mealsOk: boolean;
      exchangesOk: boolean; databaseOk: boolean; recipesOk: boolean; alternativesOk: boolean; realValuesOk: boolean;
    };
    criticalBlockers: string[];
  };
  weeklyStats: {
    averageDailyCalories: number; repeatCountByRecipe: Record<string, number>; uniqueRecipeCount?: number;
    estimatedCookCount?: number; prepTimeSavingsPercent?: number; portableMealCount: number; warnings: string[];
  };
  calorieTolerancePercent: number; maxRecipeRepeatPerWeek: number;
  varietyStrategy: RecipeVarietyStrategy; varietyScore: number; createdAt: string; updatedAt: string;
}

  | 'Ana Yemekler' | 'Salatalar & Bowllar' | 'Tatlı & Sağlıklı Kaçamak' | 'İçecek & Smoothie' | 'Sos & Garnitür';

  | 'Standart (STD)' | 'STD' | 'Glutensiz (GF)' | 'GF' | 'Laktozsuz (LF)' | 'LF'

export type CompactSeedTuple = [
  id: string, name: string, category: string, family: string, prepTimeMinutes: number,
  cookTimeMinutes: number | undefined, practicality: Practicality, transportability: Transportability,
  planCount: number, mealType: MealType, mealTypes: string[], dietType: DietType, dietTypes: string[],
  tags: string[], exchangeSummary: string, energyDisplay: string, imageUrl: string, portionSub: string,
  clinicalNote: string, kitchenNotes: string | undefined,
  detailedIngredients: [name: string, amountDisplay: string, foodId?: string][],
  steps: string[], totalNutrition: [calories: number, protein: number, carbs: number, fat: number, fiber: number],
  exchanges: [protein: number, carb: number, fat: number, vegetable: number, fruit: number, dairy: number],
  seasonMonths?: string[]
];

export function defineSeedRecipes(tuples: CompactSeedTuple[]): Recipe[] {
  return tuples.map(([
    id, name, category, family, prepTimeMinutes, cookTimeMinutes, practicality, transportability,
    planCount, mealType, mealTypes, dietType, dietTypes, tags, exchangeSummary, energyDisplay,
    imageUrl, portionSub, clinicalNote, kitchenNotes, detailedIngredients, steps,
    [calories, protein, carbs, fat, fiber], [exP, exC, exF, exV, exFr, exD], seasonMonths = ['Tüm Yıl']
  ]) => ({
    id, name, category, family, prepTimeMinutes, ...(cookTimeMinutes !== undefined ? { cookTimeMinutes } : {}),
    practicality, transportability, planCount, servings: 1, mealType, mealTypes, dietType, dietTypes,
    tags, exchangeSummary, energyDisplay, imageUrl, portionSub, clinicalNote, ...(kitchenNotes ? { kitchenNotes } : {}),
    detailedIngredients: detailedIngredients.map(([ingName, amountDisplay, foodId], idx) => ({
      order: idx + 1, name: ingName, amountDisplay, ...(foodId ? { foodId } : {})
    })),
    steps: steps.map((instruction, idx) => ({ stepNumber: idx + 1, instruction })),
    ingredients: [], totalNutrition: { calories, protein, carbs, fat, fiber },
    exchanges: { protein: exP, carb: exC, fat: exF, vegetable: exV, fruit: exFr, dairy: exD },
    seasonMonths, isDraft: false, version: '1.0', createdAt: '2026-03-20T10:00:00.000Z', updatedAt: '2026-03-27T10:00:00.000Z'
  }));
}

// [39/41] DOSYA: src/utils/nutritionCalculator.ts (153 satır)

import { FoodItem, Recipe, RecipeExchanges, RecipeIngredient, RecipeTotalNutrition } from '../types/nutrition';

export function calculateIngredientNutrition(food: FoodItem, amount: number, unit: string) {
  const unitFactor = food.unitWeightGrams[unit] || 1;
  const totalGrams = Math.max(0, amount) * unitFactor;
  const ratio = totalGrams / 100;
  return {
    grams: Math.round(totalGrams * 10) / 10,
    calories: Math.round(food.per100g.calories * ratio),
    protein: Math.round(food.per100g.protein * ratio * 10) / 10,
    carbs: Math.round(food.per100g.carbs * ratio * 10) / 10,
    fat: Math.round(food.per100g.fat * ratio * 10) / 10,
    fiber: Math.round(food.per100g.fiber * ratio * 10) / 10
  };
}

export function calculateRecipeTotalNutrition(ingredients: RecipeIngredient[]): RecipeTotalNutrition {
  let calories = 0, protein = 0, carbs = 0, fat = 0, fiber = 0;
  for (const ing of ingredients) {
    calories += ing.calculatedCalories || 0;
    protein += ing.calculatedProtein || 0;
    carbs += ing.calculatedCarbs || 0;
    fat += ing.calculatedFat || 0;
    fiber += ing.calculatedFiber || 0;
  }
  return {
    calories: Math.round(calories),
    protein: Math.round(protein * 10) / 10,
    carbs: Math.round(carbs * 10) / 10,
    fat: Math.round(fat * 10) / 10,
    fiber: Math.round(fiber * 10) / 10
  };
}

export function estimateExchangesFromIngredients(ingredients: RecipeIngredient[], foodDatabase: FoodItem[]): RecipeExchanges {
  let proteinExc = 0, carbExc = 0, fatExc = 0, vegExc = 0, fruitExc = 0, dairyExc = 0;
  const foodMap = new Map(foodDatabase.map(f => [f.id, f]));

  for (const ing of ingredients) {
    const food = foodMap.get(ing.foodId);
    const grams = (ing.amount || 0) * (food?.unitWeightGrams?.[ing.unit] || 1);
    const ratio = grams / 100;

    if (food?.exchangeContributionPer100g) {
      const c = food.exchangeContributionPer100g;
      if (c.protein) proteinExc += c.protein * ratio;
      if (c.carb) carbExc += c.carb * ratio;
      if (c.fat) fatExc += c.fat * ratio;
      if (c.vegetable) vegExc += c.vegetable * ratio;
      if (c.fruit) fruitExc += c.fruit * ratio;
      if (c.dairy) dairyExc += c.dairy * ratio;
    } else {
      const category = food?.category || '';
      const foodName = (ing.foodName || food?.name || '').toLowerCase();
      if (category === 'Süt & Süt Ürünleri' || /süt|yoğurt|kefir|ayran/.test(foodName)) {
        const dp = (ing.calculatedProtein || 0) / 6;
        dairyExc += dp > 0 ? dp : grams / 200;
      } else if (category === 'Et, Tavuk & Balık' || /somon|köfte|tavuk|eti|balık|yumurta|peynir/.test(foodName)) {
        const pp = (ing.calculatedProtein || 0) / 6;
        proteinExc += pp > 0 ? pp : grams / 30;
      } else if (category === 'Sebzeler' || /sebze|kabak|ıspanak|domates|salatalık|biber|brokoli|patlıcan/.test(foodName)) {
        vegExc += grams / 150 > 0 ? grams / 150 : 0.5;
      } else if (category === 'Meyveler' || /elma|muz|çilek|portakal|ananas|meyve/.test(foodName)) {
        const fc = (ing.calculatedCarbs || 0) / 15;
        fruitExc += fc > 0 ? fc : grams / 120;
      } else if (category === 'Yağlar & Yağlı Tohumlar' || /zeytinyağı|yağ|ceviz|badem|fındık|avokado/.test(foodName)) {
        const ff = (ing.calculatedFat || 0) / 5;
        fatExc += ff > 0 ? ff : grams / 10;
      } else {
        const cm = (ing.calculatedCarbs || 0) / 15, pm = (ing.calculatedProtein || 0) / 6, fm = (ing.calculatedFat || 0) / 5;
        if (cm >= 0.3) carbExc += cm;
        else if (pm >= 0.3) proteinExc += pm;
        else if (fm >= 0.3) fatExc += fm;
      }
    }
  }
  const roundHalf = (n: number) => Math.max(0, Math.round(n * 2) / 2);
  return {
    protein: roundHalf(proteinExc), carb: roundHalf(carbExc), fat: roundHalf(fatExc),
    vegetable: roundHalf(vegExc), fruit: roundHalf(fruitExc), dairy: roundHalf(dairyExc)
  };
}

export const DEFAULT_MACRO_RATIOS = { proteinPct: 20, carbPct: 50, fatPct: 30 };

export function calculateTargetMacroGrams(targetCalories: number, ratios = DEFAULT_MACRO_RATIOS) {
  const safeCalories = Math.max(500, targetCalories);
  return {
    calories: safeCalories,
    proteinG: Number(((safeCalories * (ratios.proteinPct / 100)) / 4).toFixed(1)),
    carbG: Number(((safeCalories * (ratios.carbPct / 100)) / 4).toFixed(1)),
    fatG: Number(((safeCalories * (ratios.fatPct / 100)) / 9).toFixed(1)),
    fiberG: Math.round(safeCalories / 70)
  };
}

export function validateRecipeIntegrity(recipe: Recipe): { isValid: boolean; error?: string } {
  if (!recipe.totalNutrition || recipe.totalNutrition.calories <= 0) {
    return { isValid: false, error: `"${recipe.name}" tarifinin kalori verisi eksik veya 0 kcal.` };
  }
  const { calories, protein, carbs, fat } = recipe.totalNutrition;
  const calculatedCal = protein * 4 + carbs * 4 + fat * 9;
  const diff = Math.abs(calculatedCal - calories);
  if (calories > 80 && diff > 80 && diff / calories > 0.35) {
    return { isValid: false, error: `"${recipe.name}" tarifinin kayıtlı enerjisi (${calories} kcal) ile makrolardan hesaplanan enerjisi (${Math.round(calculatedCal)} kcal) arasında kritik sapma var.` };
  }
  return { isValid: true };
}

export function evaluateAlternativeCategory(
  mainItem: { name: string; calories: number; protein: number; carbs: number; fat: number },
  altItem: { name: string; calories: number; protein: number; carbs: number; fat: number }
): { type: 'equivalent' | 'macro_different'; warning?: string } {
  const calRatio = mainItem.calories > 0 ? Math.abs(mainItem.calories - altItem.calories) / mainItem.calories : 0;
  const fatRatio = mainItem.fat > 0 ? Math.abs(mainItem.fat - altItem.fat) / mainItem.fat : 0;
  const protDiff = Math.abs(mainItem.protein - altItem.protein);
  if (calRatio <= 0.12 && fatRatio <= 0.25 && protDiff <= 5) return { type: 'equivalent' };
  return { type: 'macro_different', warning: 'Bu alternatif seçildiğinde günlük makro ve enerji değerleri değişebilir.' };
}

export function checkMacroCompliance(
  target: { calories: number; proteinG: number; carbG: number; fatG: number },
  actual: { calories: number; protein: number; carbs: number; fat: number }
) {
  const calc = (act: number, tgt: number, roundInt = false) => {
    const diff = roundInt ? Math.round(act - tgt) : Number((act - tgt).toFixed(1));
    const pct = tgt > 0 ? Number(((diff / tgt) * 100).toFixed(1)) : 0;
    return { diff, pct };
  };
  const e = calc(actual.calories, target.calories, true);
  const p = calc(actual.protein, target.proteinG);
  const c = calc(actual.carbs, target.carbG);
  const f = calc(actual.fat, target.fatG);

  const isEnergyCompliant = Math.abs(e.pct) <= 5.0;
  const isProteinCompliant = Math.abs(p.pct) <= 10.0;
  const isCarbCompliant = Math.abs(c.pct) <= 10.0;
  const isFatCompliant = Math.abs(f.pct) <= 10.0;
  const isAllCompliant = isEnergyCompliant && isProteinCompliant && isCarbCompliant && isFatCompliant;

  const messages: string[] = [];
  if (!isEnergyCompliant) messages.push(`Enerji sapması: %${Math.abs(e.pct)} (${e.diff > 0 ? '+' : ''}${e.diff} kcal, limit: ±%5).`);
  if (!isProteinCompliant) messages.push(`Protein sapması: %${Math.abs(p.pct)} (${p.diff > 0 ? '+' : ''}${p.diff}g, limit: ±%10).`);
  if (!isCarbCompliant) messages.push(`Karbonhidrat sapması: %${Math.abs(c.pct)} (${c.diff > 0 ? '+' : ''}${c.diff}g, limit: ±%10).`);
  if (!isFatCompliant) messages.push(`Yağ sapması: %${Math.abs(f.pct)} (${f.diff > 0 ? '+' : ''}${f.diff}g, limit: ±%10).`);

  return {
    isEnergyCompliant, isProteinCompliant, isCarbCompliant, isFatCompliant, isAllCompliant,
    energyDiff: e.diff, energyPctDiff: e.pct, proteinDiff: p.diff, proteinPctDiff: p.pct,
    carbDiff: c.diff, carbPctDiff: c.pct, fatDiff: f.diff, fatPctDiff: f.pct,
    messages, needsReoptimization: !isAllCompliant
  };
}
